通过数字 PCR 区分废水中的禽病毒和 季节性流感 病毒。

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR.
作者： Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
摘要中文翻译高致病性甲型禽流感 (H5Nx) 病毒,特别是进化枝 2.3.4.4b,对全球健康构成重大风险。我们组合并试验了 2 个双链逆转录数字 PCR,以区分废水中的禽类病毒和 季节性流感 病毒。这种方法能够实现快速、毒株特异性检测,以减少流感病毒环境 监测 的模糊性。

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR.

Authors: Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, pose substantial global health risks. We combined and piloted 2 duplex reverse transcription digital PCRs to discriminate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater. This approach enabled rapid, strain-specific detection to reduce ambiguity in environmental surveillance for influenza viruses.

审计基于模型的 抗病毒药物 建议:季节性流感 监测 的决策相关性诊断

Auditing model-based antiviral recommendations: a decision-relevance diagnostic for seasonal influenza surveillance
作者： Tian-Yu Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 抗病毒药物 库存决策取决于根据 监测 数据校准的 传播 模型。季节性流感 监测 记录报告病例而不是感染情况,因此报告率 ρ 和初始感染规模 i 0 仅通过其乘积进入预期计数:可能性沿着 (ρ,i 0 ) 中的山脊完全平坦。标准校准检查可以通过,而建议的干预措施仍未确定。方法我们建立了一个具有负二项式报告的六参数SEIRS型流感模型,将神经后验估计器与报告的计数进行拟合,并使用方差加权决策相关性评分(DRS)对决策稳定性进行评分,DRS是来自弱识别方向的决策边际后验方差的份额。尺度不变的阳性对照检查了分数。我们审核了 192 个单元的合成网格和 204 个真实的 CDC 流感区域季节,将分数与纯几何替代方案进行比较。结果 岭是精确的:在 ρ 的整个先验范围内补偿 i 0 最多可将单季对数似然改变 0.261。在合成网格上,有界后验翻转了 27% 的单元格中的插件操作,损失了 12% 的预言机价值。几何指数未能达到其自己的控制臂,而方差加权得分将各臂分开(0.30 vs 0.13,Fisher/Laplace 几何;0.25 vs 0.05,神经后验)。参数级不可识别性并不能预测决策不稳定(Spearman + 0.107,p = 0.14)。在实际季节中,敏感决策与不敏感决策的得分相差高达数百倍,204 个地区季节中有 7 个 (3.4%) 的差距低于其自身的后验标准差,其中 2022-2023 年季节有 6 个。结论 校准后验不足以证明基于模型的推荐已被识别,并且参数级诊断无法揭示何时发生这种情况。根据球队已经拥有的后验计算并且不需要新数据,分数标记了推荐不可信的赛季;我们建议将其作为例行审核与校准检查一起运行。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决根据 季节性流感 监测 数据确定可靠的 抗病毒药物 建议的挑战,这些数据通常缺乏足够的信息来区分报告的病例和实际感染。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 192 个单元格和 204 个真实 CDC 流感区域季节的合成网格,比较不同数据场景中基于模型的 抗病毒药物 建议。
核心方法
研究人员开发了一种具有负二项式报告的六参数 SEIRS 型流感模型,将神经后验估计器拟合到报告的计数,并使用方差加权决策相关性评分 (DRS) 来评估决策稳定性。
主要结果
结果表明,似然函数沿着山脊平坦,这意味着改变报告率 ρ 和初始感染大小 i 0 对对数似然的影响最小。在合成网格上,有界后验翻转了 27% 的单元格中的插件操作,损失了 12% 的预言机价值。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明仅校准后验不足以确认基于模型的建议已被识别,并且参数级别的不可识别性并不一定会导致决策不稳定。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,所提出的决策相关性评分可以用作常规审核,以识别 抗病毒药物 建议可能不可靠的季节,从而提高 季节性流感 监测 和决策的准确性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括依赖合成网格和真实的疾病预防控制中心数据,这可能无法完全代表所有流感动态。证据强度中等,因为结果是基于模拟和真实数据的组合。

Auditing model-based antiviral recommendations: a decision-relevance diagnostic for seasonal influenza surveillance

Authors: Tian-Yu Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Antiviral stockpile decisions rest on transmission models calibrated to surveillance data. Seasonal influenza surveillance records reported cases rather than infections, so the reporting rate ρ and the initial infection size i 0 enter expected counts only through their product: the likelihood is exactly flat along a ridge in (ρ, i 0 ). Standard calibration checks can pass while the recommended intervention remains unidentified. Methods We built a six-parameter SEIRS-type influenza model with negative-binomial reporting, fit neural posterior estimators to reported counts, and scored decision stability with a variance-weighted decision-relevance score (DRS), the share of the decision margin's posterior variance coming from weakly identified directions. A scale-invariant positive control checked the score. We audited a 192-cell synthetic grid and 204 real CDC influenza region-seasons, comparing the score with a purely geometric alternative. Results The ridge is exact: compensating i 0 across ρ's full prior range changes the single-season log-likelihood by at most 0.261. On the synthetic grid the bounded posterior flipped the plug-in action in 27% of cells, losing 12% of the oracle's value. The geometric index failed its own control arm, whereas the variance-weighted score separated the arms (0.30 vs 0.13, Fisher/Laplace geometry; 0.25 vs 0.05, neural posterior). Parameter-level non-identifiability did not predict decision instability (Spearman + 0.107, p = 0.14). On real seasons the score separated sensitive from insensitive decisions by up to several hundredfold, and seven of 204 region-seasons (3.4%) had a margin below its own posterior standard deviation, six in the 2022–2023 season. Conclusions A calibrated posterior is not sufficient evidence that a model-based recommendation is identified, and parameter-level diagnostics do not reveal when this happens. Computed from the posterior a team already holds and needing no new data, the score flags the seasons in which the recommendation would be untrustworthy; we suggest running it as a routine audit alongside calibration checks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of identifying reliable antiviral recommendations based on seasonal influenza surveillance data, which often lacks sufficient information to distinguish between reported cases and actual infections.
Design and population
The study involved a synthetic grid of 192 cells and 204 real CDC influenza region-seasons, comparing model-based antiviral recommendations across different data scenarios.
Core methods
The researchers developed a six-parameter SEIRS-type influenza model with negative-binomial reporting, fitted neural posterior estimators to reported counts, and used a variance-weighted decision-relevance score (DRS) to assess decision stability.
Main results
The results showed that the likelihood function is flat along a ridge, meaning that varying the reporting rate ρ and initial infection size i 0 has minimal impact on the log-likelihood. On the synthetic grid, the bounded posterior flipped the plug-in action in 27% of cells, losing 12% of the oracle's value.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that a calibrated posterior alone is insufficient to confirm model-based recommendations are identified, and that parameter-level non-identifiability does not necessarily lead to decision instability.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the proposed decision-relevance score can be used as a routine audit to identify seasons where antiviral recommendations may be unreliable, thereby improving the accuracy of seasonal influenza surveillance and decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the reliance on a synthetic grid and real CDC data, which may not fully represent all influenza dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on a combination of simulated and real-world data.

欧亚类禽H1N1猪流感病毒时空基因组流行病学

Spatiotemporal genomic epidemiology of Eurasian avian-like H1N1 swine influenza viruses
作者： Hanlin Liu, Yao Zhang, Jiangnan Yi, Zhijian Zhou, Chunyuan Li, Fengxiang Xu, Ruiwen Ren, Ming Liao, Hailiang Sun
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 欧亚类禽 (EA) H1N1 猪流感病毒构成人畜共患威胁,但其基因组 进化 和在欧亚大陆的传播仍不完全清楚。本研究调查了基因型多样性、时间持久性和地理传播,并评估了活猪贸易和病毒迁移之间的关联,为 监测 提供信息。方法 我们分析了 2009 年至 2025 年间从 19 个国家采样的 1,703 个公开的 EA H1N1 基因组。所有八个基因片段的谱系分配用于识别基因型并描述其时间和地理分布。时间尺度的贝叶斯系统发育学和离散系统发育地理学被用来重建进化关系和迁移模式。广义线性模型评估了迁移预测因素,包括定向活猪贸易和采样强度。进化 的混合效应模型用于检测血凝素中的情景多样化选择,然后对候选残基进行结构作图。结果 我们鉴定出 19 种基因型。完全源自 EA 的基因型 G1 占 基因组 的 57.0%,并且几乎在整个研究期间都被检测到。许多携带 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 或三重重配衍生片段的重配基因型在短时间内被检测到,尽管有几种重配基因型表现出长期持续性。基因型组成因地理位置而异,重配基因型在亚洲丰富,而主要源自 EA 的基因型在欧洲更为常见。系统发育地理学重建表明欧洲和亚洲内部和之间的传播不对称,西欧在推断的迁徙网络中占据中心位置。在考虑抽样强度后,定向活猪贸易在迁徙预测因素中获得了最有力的支持。十六个候选血凝素密码子显示出情景多样化选择的证据。大多数候选 HA1 残基定位到靠近抗原区域的球状头的暴露区域。结论 欧亚大陆的 EA H1N1 进化 的特点是持续的 EA 基因组背景、通过重配实现的区域基因型多样化以及与活猪贸易相关的传播。这些发现支持将 基因组监测 与动物移动数据相结合,以指导 监测 沿贸易网络发展,并改进对人畜共患问题的新变种的检测和评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查基因组 进化 和欧亚类禽 (EA) H1N1 猪流感病毒在欧亚大陆的传播,解决对其人畜共患病威胁和基因组 进化 的不完全了解。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2009 年至 2025 年间来自 19 个国家的 1,703 个 EA H1N1 基因组,重点关注基因型多样性、时间持久性和地理传播,特别强调活猪贸易和病毒迁移之间的关联。
核心方法
Genotype assignments across all eight gene segments were used to identify genotypes and describe their temporal and geographic distributions. Time-scaled Bayesian phylogenetics and discrete phylogeography were employed to reconstruct evolutionary relationships and migration patterns. Generalized linear modeling assessed migration predictors,including directed live-swine trade and sampling intensity. 进化 的混合效应模型用于检测血凝素中的情景多样化选择,然后对候选残基进行结构作图。
主要结果
该研究鉴定出 19 种基因型,其中完全源自 EA 的基因型 G1 占 基因组 的 57.0%,并且几乎在整个研究期间都被检测到。许多重配基因型在短时间内被检测到,显示出长期的持续性。基因型组成因地理位置而异,重配基因型在亚洲丰富,而主要源自 EA 的基因型在欧洲更为常见。在考虑抽样强度后,定向活猪贸易是迁徙预测因素中最有力的支持。16 个候选血凝素密码子显示出情景多样化选择的证据,大多数候选 HA1 残基映射到靠近抗原区域的球状头的暴露区域。
解释与创新
研究结果支持将 基因组监测 与动物移动数据相结合,以指导 监测 沿贸易网络发展,并改进对人畜共患新出现变异的检测和评估,强调活猪贸易在病毒迁移中的作用以及通过重配实现区域基因型多样化的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果可以通过将基因组数据与动物运动模式相结合,为 监测 策略提供信息,从而增强对新出现的人畜共患流感变异的检测和评估。这有助于及早识别潜在的大流行威胁并指导公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括依赖公开的 基因组、潜在的抽样偏差以及需要进一步验证已识别的候选残基。由于该研究的观察性质以及需要纵向数据来确认活猪贸易在病毒迁移中的作用,证据强度中等。

Spatiotemporal genomic epidemiology of Eurasian avian-like H1N1 swine influenza viruses

Authors: Hanlin Liu, Yao Zhang, Jiangnan Yi, Zhijian Zhou, Chunyuan Li, Fengxiang Xu, Ruiwen Ren, Ming Liao, Hailiang Sun
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Eurasian avian-like (EA) H1N1 swine influenza viruses pose a zoonotic threat, yet their genomic evolution and dissemination across Eurasia remain incompletely understood. This study investigated genotype diversity, temporal persistence, and geographic spread, and assessed the association between live-swine trade and viral migration to inform surveillance. Methods We analysed 1,703 publicly available EA H1N1 genomes sampled from 19 countries between 2009 and 2025. Lineage assignments across all eight gene segments were used to identify genotypes and describe their temporal and geographic distributions. Time-scaled Bayesian phylogenetics and discrete phylogeography were used to reconstruct evolutionary relationships and migration patterns. Generalized linear modelling assessed migration predictors, including directed live-swine trade and sampling intensity. The mixed effects model of evolution was used to detect episodic diversifying selection in hemagglutinin, followed by structural mapping of candidate residues. Results We identified 19 genotypes. The wholly EA-derived genotype G1 accounted for 57.0% of genomes and was detected across nearly the entire study period. Many reassortant genotypes carrying A(H1N1)pdm09- or triple-reassortant-derived segments were detected over short intervals, although several showed prolonged persistence. Genotype composition differed geographically, with reassortant genotypes enriched in Asia and predominantly EA-derived constellations more common in Europe. Phylogeographic reconstruction indicated asymmetric dissemination within and between Europe and Asia, with Western Europe occupying a central position in the inferred migration network. Directed live-swine trade received the strongest support among migration predictors after accounting for sampling intensity. Sixteen candidate hemagglutinin codons showed evidence of episodic diversifying selection. Most candidate HA1 residues mapped to exposed regions of the globular head near antigenic regions. Conclusions EA H1N1 evolution across Eurasia was characterized by a persistent EA genomic background, regional genotype diversification through reassortment, and spread associated with live-swine trade. These findings support integrating genomic surveillance with animal movement data to guide surveillance along trade networks and improve the detection and assessment of emerging variants of zoonotic concern.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the genomic evolution and dissemination of Eurasian avian-like (EA) H1N1 swine influenza viruses across Eurasia, addressing the incomplete understanding of their zoonotic threat and genomic evolution.
Design and population
The study analyzed 1,703 EA H1N1 genomes from 19 countries between 2009 and 2025, focusing on genotype diversity, temporal persistence, and geographic spread, with a particular emphasis on the association between live-swine trade and viral migration.
Core methods
Genotype assignments across all eight gene segments were used to identify genotypes and describe their temporal and geographic distributions. Time-scaled Bayesian phylogenetics and discrete phylogeography were employed to reconstruct evolutionary relationships and migration patterns. Generalized linear modeling assessed migration predictors, including directed live-swine trade and sampling intensity. The mixed effects model of evolution was used to detect episodic diversifying selection in hemagglutinin, followed by structural mapping of candidate residues.
Main results
The study identified 19 genotypes, with the wholly EA-derived genotype G1 accounting for 57.0% of genomes and being detected across nearly the entire study period. Many reassortant genotypes were detected over short intervals, showing prolonged persistence. Genotype composition differed geographically, with reassortant genotypes enriched in Asia and predominantly EA-derived constellations more common in Europe. Directed live-swine trade was the strongest support among migration predictors after accounting for sampling intensity. Sixteen candidate hemagglutinin codons showed evidence of episodic diversifying selection, with most candidate HA1 residues mapping to exposed regions of the globular head near antigenic regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support integrating genomic surveillance with animal movement data to guide surveillance along trade networks and improve the detection and assessment of emerging variants of zoonotic concern, highlighting the role of live-swine trade in viral migration and the importance of regional genotype diversification through reassortment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study's findings can inform surveillance strategies by integrating genomic data with animal movement patterns, enhancing the detection and assessment of emerging zoonotic influenza variants. This can help in the early identification of potential pandemic threats and guide public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the reliance on publicly available genomes, potential sampling biases, and the need for further validation of the identified candidate residues. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the need for longitudinal data to confirm the role of live-swine trade in viral migration.

肿瘤内甲型流感病毒感染驱动非小细胞肺癌的铂类耐药和侵入性重编程

Intratumoral influenza A virus infection drives platinum resistance and invasive reprogramming in NSCLC
作者： Isabelle Cremer, Pierre-Emmanuel Foy, Nathalie Josseaume, Piya Cagdas, Florian Houdu, Emma Fleury, Thierno Balde, Hadrien Guichard, Solenne Marmier, Katerina Kalkusova
摘要中文翻译<title>Abstract</title> <p>呼吸道病毒感染在非小细胞肺癌(NSCLC)患者中很常见,但瘤内病毒的存在是否直接影响肿瘤行为和治疗反应仍不清楚。在这里,我们确定了一种呼吸道病毒相关药物代谢程序(REVIS-DM),该程序主要由肿瘤细胞表达,并与不良的临床结果相关。在多种 NSCLC 队列研究 中,同时存在高 REVIS-DM、干扰素刺激基因和 抗病毒药物 特征的肿瘤亚组与化疗后生存率降低和复发增加相关。与此 抗病毒药物 概况一致,在 NSCLC 中检测到甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 转录物,并与不良预后和复发相关。从机制上讲,IAV感染在体外和体内诱导异生物质解毒、上皮间质转化、侵袭和铂耐药。这些效应需要病毒传感和 NLRP3 炎性体信号传导,但独立于 I/III 型干扰素途径。总之,我们的研究结果表明,肿瘤内 IAV 感染是 NSCLC 肿瘤细胞可塑性和化疗耐药性的一个先前未被认识的驱动因素,揭示了病毒诱导的代谢脆弱性,具有潜在的治疗相关性。</p>

Intratumoral influenza A virus infection drives platinum resistance and invasive reprogramming in NSCLC

Authors: Isabelle Cremer, Pierre-Emmanuel Foy, Nathalie Josseaume, Piya Cagdas, Florian Houdu, Emma Fleury, Thierno Balde, Hadrien Guichard, Solenne Marmier, Katerina Kalkusova
Original abstract (English)
<title>Abstract</title> <p>Respiratory viral infections are frequent in patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), yet whether intratumoral viral presence directly influences tumor behavior and therapeutic response remains unknown. Here, we identify a respiratory virus-associated drug metabolism program (REVIS-DM), predominantly expressed by tumor cells and linked to poor clinical outcome. Across multiple NSCLC cohorts, a subgroup of tumors with concurrent high REVIS-DM, interferon-stimulated gene and antiviral signatures was associated with reduced survival and increased relapse after chemotherapy. Consistent with this antiviral profile, Influenza A virus (IAV) transcripts were detected in NSCLC and associated with poor outcome and relapse. Mechanistically, IAV infection induced xenobiotic detoxification, epithelial-to-mesenchymal transition, invasion, and platinum resistance in vitro and in vivo. These effects required viral sensing and NLRP3 inflammasome signaling but were independent of type I/III interferon pathways. Together, our findings establish intratumoral IAV infection as a previously unrecognized driver of tumor-cell plasticity and chemoresistance in NSCLC, revealing virus-induced metabolic vulnerabilities with potential therapeutic relevance.</p>

Question and background
Respiratory viral infections are frequent in patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), yet whether intratumoral viral presence directly influences tumor behavior and therapeutic response remains unknown.
Design and population
Here, we identify a respiratory virus-associated drug metabolism program (REVIS-DM), predominantly expressed by tumor cells and linked to poor clinical outcome.
Core methods

Main results
Consistent with this antiviral profile, Influenza A virus (IAV) transcripts were detected in NSCLC and associated with poor outcome and relapse.
Interpretation and novelty
Mechanistically, IAV infection induced xenobiotic detoxification, epithelial-to-mesenchymal transition, invasion, and platinum resistance in vitro and in vivo.
Scientific and public-health significance
These effects required viral sensing and NLRP3 inflammasome signaling but were independent of type I/III interferon pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
Together, our findings establish intratumoral IAV infection as a previously unrecognized driver of tumor-cell plasticity and chemoresistance in NSCLC, revealing virus-induced metabolic vulnerabilities with potential therapeutic relevance.

红火龙果甜菜苷在甲型流感病毒感染期间选择性上调体外促炎细胞因子。

Red Pitahaya Betacyanins Selectively Upregulate Proinflammatory Cytokines In Vitro During Influenza A Virus Infection.
作者： Lim CM, Mat Isa N, Omar AR, Choo WS
摘要中文翻译在四种流感类型中,甲型流感病毒(IAV)每年都会流行和大流行,引起最严重的疾病,导致患者住院或死亡。随着 IAV 威胁下一次全球流感大流行,寻找 抗病毒药物 药物是一场与时间的赛跑。甜菜青苷是一种红紫色、独特的水溶性含氮色素,此前曾有报道称其具有 抗病毒药物 抗 IAV 作用。本研究的目的是研究 IAV 感染的 A549 细胞中甜菜青素与关键炎症标志物之间的相互作用,例如白细胞介素 (IL)-1 β、IL-6、肿瘤坏死因子 (TNF)-α 和细胞因子信号传导抑制因子 3 (SOCS3)。首先从红火龙果中提取甜菜青苷,并对其进行定量和表征。A549 细胞被 IAV 感染,然后用甜菜苷处理。进行定量PCR来研究IL-1β、IL-6、TNF-α和SOCS3表达水平的变化。进行分子对接以研究甜菜花青素与 IL-1 β、IL-6、TNF-α、SOCS3 及其各自受体(即 IL-1R1、IL-6R 和 TNFR1)之间的相互作用。甜菜青素在长达 72 小时后独特地调节 IL-1 β、IL-6 和 TNF-α。具体而言,IL-1β和IL-6在48和72小时后均显着下调,TNF-α在48小时后显着下调。此外,SOCS3(IAV 劫持并诱导以促进其自身生存的 宿主 因子)在 48 小时和 72 小时后下调。计算机分析表明,甜菜青素(如叶菌素、hylocerenin、甜菜碱和异甜菜碱)与 IL-1β、IL-6、TNF-α、SOCS3、IL-1R1、IL-6R 和 TNFR1 形成结合相互作用和氢键。这项研究表明,β花青素和炎症细胞因子之间的新型相互作用可能有助于β花青素的 抗病毒药物 效应,从而导致 宿主 细胞的存活。建议未来对细胞的免疫学方面和整体细胞健康进行研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 会导致严重疾病、住院和死亡,并且有可能引发下一次全球大流行,因此迫切需要确定新的 抗病毒药物 策略。该研究旨在研究 IAV 感染细胞中甜菜苷与关键炎症标志物之间的相互作用。
设计与对象
该研究是在体外使用感染甲型流感病毒的 A549 细胞进行的,然后用从红火龙果中提取的β花青素进行处理。目的是检查 IAV 感染期间炎症细胞因子和 宿主 因子的调节。
核心方法
从红火龙果中提取、定量并表征甜菜花苷。A549 细胞被 IAV 感染并用甜菜苷处理。采用定量PCR检测IL-1β、IL-6、TNF-α和SOCS3表达水平的变化。进行分子对接以分析甜菜青素与其靶标之间的相互作用。
主要结果
甜菜青素在长达 72 小时后独特地调节 IL-1 β、IL-6 和 TNF-α。具体而言,IL-1β和IL-6在48和72小时后均显着下调,TNF-α在48小时后显着下调。48 小时和 72 小时后 SOCS3 也下调。
解释与创新
研究表明,β花青素可能与炎症细胞因子和 宿主 因子相互作用,通过调节这些途径可能有助于 抗病毒药物 效应和 宿主 细胞存活。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为针对 季节性流感 炎症反应的新型 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息,为改善 宿主 针对 IAV 的防御机制提供潜在途径。
局限与证据强度
该研究是使用单细胞系在体外进行的,这可能限制其对人类感染的普遍适用性。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于体外实验和计算分析而没有体内验证。

Red Pitahaya Betacyanins Selectively Upregulate Proinflammatory Cytokines In Vitro During Influenza A Virus Infection.

Authors: Lim CM, Mat Isa N, Omar AR, Choo WS
Original abstract (English)
Among the four influenza types, Influenza A virus (IAV) is responsible for annual epidemics and pandemics, causing the most severe illnesses, resulting in patient hospitalizations or death. With IAV threatening the next global influenza pandemic, it is a race against time to search for antiviral drugs. Betacyanins are reddish-violet, unique water-soluble nitrogen-containing pigments that have previously been reported to possess antiviral effects against IAV. The objectives of this study were to study interactions between betacyanins and key inflammatory markers, such as interleukin (IL)-1 β , IL-6, tumor necrosis factor (TNF)- α , and suppressor of cytokine signaling 3 (SOCS3), in IAV-infected A549 cells. Betacyanins were first extracted from red pitahaya, quantified, and characterized. A549 cells were infected with IAV and then treated with betacyanins. Quantitative PCR was performed to study the change in expression levels of IL-1 β , IL-6, TNF- α , and SOCS3. Molecular docking was performed to study any interactions between betacyanins and IL-1 β , IL-6, TNF- α , SOCS3, and their respective receptors, namely, IL-1R1, IL-6R, and TNFR1. Betacyanins modulated IL-1 β , IL-6, and TNF- α uniquely after up to 72 h. Specifically, both IL-1 β and IL-6 were significantly downregulated after 48 and 72 h, and TNF- α was significantly downregulated after 48 h. Additionally, SOCS3, a host factor that IAV hijacks and induces to promote its own survival, was downregulated after 48 and 72 h. In silico analysis showed that betacyanins such as phyllocactin, hylocerenin, betanin, and isobetanin formed binding interactions and hydrogen bonds with IL-1 β , IL-6, TNF- α , SOCS3, IL-1R1, IL-6R, and TNFR1. This study suggests that novel interactions between betacyanins and inflammatory cytokines might contribute to the antiviral effects of betacyanins, leading to the survival of the host cell. Future studies into the immunological aspects of the cell and overall cellular health are recommended.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus (IAV) causes severe illnesses, hospitalizations, and deaths, and with its potential to trigger the next global pandemic, there is an urgent need to identify new antiviral strategies. The study aimed to investigate the interactions between betacyanins and key inflammatory markers in IAV-infected cells.
Design and population
The study was conducted in vitro using A549 cells infected with Influenza A virus and then treated with betacyanins extracted from red pitahaya. The objectives were to examine the modulation of inflammatory cytokines and host factors during IAV infection.
Core methods
Betacyanins were extracted, quantified, and characterized from red pitahaya. A549 cells were infected with IAV and treated with betacyanins. Quantitative PCR was used to measure changes in the expression levels of IL-1 β , IL-6, TNF- α , and SOCS3. Molecular docking was performed to analyze interactions between betacyanins and their targets.
Main results
Betacyanins modulated IL-1 β , IL-6, and TNF- α uniquely after up to 72 h. Specifically, both IL-1 β and IL-6 were significantly downregulated after 48 and 72 h, and TNF- α was significantly downregulated after 48 h. SOCS3 was also downregulated after 48 and 72 h.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that betacyanins may interact with inflammatory cytokines and host factors, potentially contributing to antiviral effects and host cell survival through modulation of these pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of novel antiviral therapies targeting inflammatory responses in seasonal influenza, offering a potential avenue for improving host defense mechanisms against IAV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro using a single cell line, which may limit its generalizability to human infections. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on in vitro experiments and computational analysis without in vivo validation.

西班牙新型重配猪甲型流感病毒的人畜共患病感染和基因组 进化

Zoonotic infections and genomic evolution of novel reassortant swine influenza A viruses in Spain
作者： Paloma A. Encinas, Brady M. O’Boyle, Alexander Maksiaev, Martha I. Nelson, Adolfo García‐Sastre, Gustavo del Real, Alexander Maksaiev, Adolfo García-Sastre
摘要中文翻译摘要 甲型流感病毒(IAV)在欧洲猪群中广泛传播,并通过频繁地从人类引入以及随后在猪体内的重配而继续多样化。由于集约化的白猪生产、广泛的伊比利亚猪系统和丰富的野猪种群并存,西班牙代表了一个特别活跃的生态环境。本研究对 2019 年至 2022 年间西班牙猪的 IAV 进化 和基因组多样性进行了综合分析,扩展了 2016 年至 2019 年之前的 监测。在 24 个省进行采样,产生了来自伊比利亚猪和白猪的 66 个新的全 基因组 序列。我们鉴定了 18 种基因型,包括我们之前的调查中未检测到的 11 种新型重配体。几种基因型,如 H1N2 G21 和 G22、H3N2 G23 以及不寻常的 H3N1 G12 是该国独有的。在托莱多和巴达霍斯的白猪、伊比利亚猪和野猪中检测到了一些基因型,表明病毒在猪群中流动。系统发育分析显示,H1N1pdm09 病毒持续从人类传入猪体内,产生至少五种重配基因型(G10、G16-G19)。这些谱系包含大流行的内部基因盒,在某些情况下,还包含人类季节性的 N2 片段,强调了人类作为病毒入侵来源的持续作用。相反,2022 年至 2026 年间在西班牙检测到的四例人畜共患感染 (H1N1v) 与白猪中传播的基因型有关,这强调了 IAV 传播 在人猪界面上的双向性质。总体而言,这项研究表明西班牙提供了有利于 IAV 多样化、重组和人畜共患病风险的生态条件。研究结果强化了持续的 One Health 监测 的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解西班牙猪体内的 进化 和甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的基因组多样性,解决由于动态的生态条件和人类频繁引入而对持续 监测 的需求。
设计与对象
该研究从 2019 年到 2022 年在西班牙 24 个省份进行了采样,重点关注伊比利亚猪、白猪和野猪,以分析 IAV 的基因组多样性。
核心方法
对 66 种新的猪 IAV 分离株进行全 基因组 测序,以鉴定基因型和重配体,并进行系统发育分析,以追踪病毒引入和重配事件。
主要结果
该研究鉴定了 18 种基因型,包括 11 种新型重配体,并发现几种基因型是西班牙独有的,随着 H1N1pdm09 不断从人类引入,产生了重配基因型。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了西班牙作为 IAV 进化 和重配动态环境的作用,确定了新的基因型和重配体,强调了继续 监测 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了持续的 One Health 监测 监测猪 IAV 进化 和 传播 的必要性,强调了 IAV 传播 在人猪界面上的双向性质。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括潜在的抽样偏差和需要更大的样本量来概括研究结果,但已确定的基因型和重配的证据是强有力的。

Zoonotic infections and genomic evolution of novel reassortant swine influenza A viruses in Spain

Authors: Paloma A. Encinas, Brady M. O’Boyle, Alexander Maksiaev, Martha I. Nelson, Adolfo García‐Sastre, Gustavo del Real, Alexander Maksaiev, Adolfo García-Sastre
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza A virus (IAV) circulates widely in European pig populations and continues to diversify through frequent introductions from humans, followed by reassortment within swine. Spain represents a particularly dynamic ecological setting due to the coexistence of intensive white-pig production, extensive Iberian-pig systems, and abundant wild boar populations. This study provides an integrated analysis of IAV evolution and genomic diversity in swine in Spain between 2019 and 2022, expanding on previous surveillance from 2016 to 2019. Sampling across 24 provinces yielded 66 new whole-genome sequences from Iberian and white pigs. We identified 18 genotypes, including 11 novel reassortants not detected in our previous survey. Several genotypes, such as H1N2 G21 and G22, H3N2 G23, and the unusual H3N1 G12, were exclusive to the country. Some genotypes were detected across white pigs, Iberian pigs, and wild boar in Toledo and Badajoz, suggesting viral flow among swine populations. Phylogenetic analyses revealed ongoing introductions of H1N1pdm09 from humans into pigs, generating at least five reassortant genotypes (G10, G16–G19). These lineages incorporated pandemic internal cassettes and, in some cases, human-seasonal N2 segments, highlighting the continued role of humans as a source of viral incursions. Conversely, four zoonotic infections (H1N1v) detected in Spain between 2022 and 2026 were linked to genotypes circulating in white pigs, underscoring the bidirectional nature of IAV transmission at the human swine interface. Overall, this study demonstrates that Spain provides ecological conditions conducive to IAV diversification, reassortment, and zoonotic risk. The findings reinforce the need for sustained One Health surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the evolution and genomic diversity of influenza A viruses (IAVs) in swine in Spain, addressing the need for sustained surveillance due to the dynamic ecological conditions and frequent introductions from humans.
Design and population
The study involved sampling across 24 provinces in Spain from 2019 to 2022, focusing on Iberian and white pigs, and wild boar, to analyze the genomic diversity of IAVs.
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing was performed on 66 new IAV isolates from swine to identify genotypes and reassortants, and phylogenetic analyses were conducted to trace viral introductions and reassortment events.
Main results
The study identified 18 genotypes, including 11 novel reassortants, and found that several genotypes were exclusive to Spain, with ongoing introductions of H1N1pdm09 from humans generating reassortant genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the role of Spain as a dynamic setting for IAV evolution and reassortment, with novel genotypes and reassortants identified, underscoring the need for continued surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study reinforces the need for sustained One Health surveillance to monitor the evolution and transmission of IAVs in swine, emphasizing the bidirectional nature of IAV transmission at the human swine interface.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the potential for sampling bias and the need for larger sample sizes to generalize findings, but the evidence is strong for the identified genotypes and reassortants.

同步共感染驱动甲型流感病毒群体的谱系代表性

Synchronous co-infection drives lineage representation in influenza A virus populations
作者： Álvaro Fajardo, Justin J. Frere, Skyler A. Uhl, Ryla Cantergiani, Leon L. Hsieh, Maryline Panis, Lucía Carrau, Benjamin Robert tenOever, Alvaro Fajardo
摘要中文翻译摘要 RNA 病毒通过复制错误进化,产生多样性,但也可以触发细胞 抗病毒药物 反应,在适应和 宿主 控制之间产生内在的张力。甲型流感病毒生物学通过 基因组 分段增加了​​这种复杂性,这使得重配成为可能,但也通过不完整的 基因组 表达和包装缺陷以及通过限制互补的双重感染排除而产生感染异质性。总之,这些限制预计将限制个体变体的持续扩张,但甲型流感病毒在本质上继续适应,这表明特定的感染环境可能会导致不成比例的谱系扩增。为了识别此类背景,我们将体外单细胞转录组学与病毒谱系追踪相结合,将受感染的细胞环境与病毒输出动态联系起来。我们发现谱系富集主要发生在同步共感染期间,此时多个病毒 基因组 占据同一细胞,并且病毒输出增加。这些发现确定了一种依赖于时间的条件,在该条件下谱系代表性可能会发生变化,并预测增加爆发输出或同步共感染的可能性的特征可能会不成比例地影响种群构成。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定甲型流感病毒群体中允许谱系扩增的感染环境,因为RNA病毒通过复制错误进化,产生多样性,但也可以触发细胞抗病毒药物反应,在适应和宿主控制之间产生固有的紧张关系。
设计与对象
该研究使用体外单细胞转录组学与病毒谱系追踪相结合,将受感染的细胞环境与病毒输出动态联系起来,重点研究甲型流感病毒生物学及其由于 基因组 分割和重复感染排除而产生的复杂性。
核心方法
研究人员将体外单细胞转录组学与病毒谱系追踪相结合,分析受感染的细胞环境与病毒输出动态之间的关系,特别检查同步共感染对谱系表征的影响。
主要结果
研究结果表明,谱系富集主要发生在同步共感染期间,此时多个病毒 基因组 占据同一细胞,并且病毒输出增加。
解释与创新
该研究解释说,谱系代表性可能会在时间依赖性条件下发生变化,并且增加爆发输出或同步合并感染的可能性的特征可能会不成比例地影响人口组成。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强调同步共感染在塑造病毒种群动态中的作用来为 监测 和 诊断 策略提供信息,这可能对理解病毒 进化 和自然环境中的适应具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,甲型流感病毒生物学通过 基因组 分割和重复感染排除增加了复杂性,这可能会限制个体变体的一致扩展,但证据是强有力的,因为它基于直接体外观察。

Synchronous co-infection drives lineage representation in influenza A virus populations

Authors: Álvaro Fajardo, Justin J. Frere, Skyler A. Uhl, Ryla Cantergiani, Leon L. Hsieh, Maryline Panis, Lucía Carrau, Benjamin Robert tenOever, Alvaro Fajardo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract RNA viruses evolve through replication errors that generate diversity but can also trigger cellular antiviral responses, creating an inherent tension between adaptation and host control. Influenza A virus biology adds to this complexity through genome segmentation, which enables reassortment but also creates infection heterogeneity through incomplete genome expression and packaging defects, and through superinfection exclusion, which restricts complementation. Together, these constraints would be expected to limit the consistent expansion of individual variants, yet the influenza A virus continues to adapt in nature, suggesting that specific infection contexts may allow disproportionate lineage amplification. To identify such contexts, we paired in vitro single-cell transcriptomics with viral lineage tracking to link infected cellular environments to viral output dynamics. We find that lineage enrichment predominantly occurs during synchronous coinfection, when multiple viral genomes occupy the same cell, and viral output is increased. These findings identify a timing-dependent condition under which lineage representation can shift, and predict that traits increasing burst output or the likelihood of synchronous coinfection may disproportionately shape population composition.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify infection contexts that allow lineage amplification in influenza A virus populations, as RNA viruses evolve through replication errors that generate diversity but can also trigger cellular antiviral responses, creating an inherent tension between adaptation and host control.
Design and population
The study used in vitro single-cell transcriptomics paired with viral lineage tracking to link infected cellular environments to viral output dynamics, focusing on influenza A virus biology and its complexity due to genome segmentation and superinfection exclusion.
Core methods
The researchers combined in vitro single-cell transcriptomics with viral lineage tracking to analyze the relationship between infected cellular environments and viral output dynamics, specifically examining the effects of synchronous coinfection on lineage representation.
Main results
The findings revealed that lineage enrichment predominantly occurs during synchronous coinfection, when multiple viral genomes occupy the same cell, and viral output is increased.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets that lineage representation can shift under timing-dependent conditions, and that traits increasing burst output or the likelihood of synchronous coinfection may disproportionately shape population composition.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform surveillance and diagnosis strategies by highlighting the role of synchronous coinfection in shaping viral population dynamics, which may have implications for understanding viral evolution and adaptation in natural settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that influenza A virus biology adds complexity through genome segmentation and superinfection exclusion, which may limit the consistent expansion of individual variants, but the evidence is strong as it is based on direct in vitro observations.

细胞类型对结合和复制的相反偏好塑造了甲型流感病毒在人类呼吸道中的感染。

Opposing cell-type preferences for binding and replication shape influenza A virus infection in human airways.
作者： von Kempis J, Pohl MO, Gaggioli E, Niklaus C, Trkola A, Boons GJ, de Vries RP, Stertz S
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒(IAV)对人类健康构成威胁。尽管受体特异性、聚合酶适应性和气道向性已被广泛研究,但它们如何结合形成气道上皮细胞的向性仍悬而未决。在这里,我们发现禽类 IAV 感染纤毛细胞和分泌细胞,但由于顶端 α2,3 连接的唾液酸水平较高,优先与纤毛细胞结合。由于细胞类型之间的复制水平相当,这转化为对纤毛细胞的总体偏好。尽管 α2,6 连接的唾液酸丰度相当,但人类 IAV 也优先与纤毛细胞结合。相反,它们在分泌细胞中表现出更高的病毒RNA和蛋白质水平,这与它们与人类ANP32蛋白的相容性以及该细胞类型中较高的ANP32A/B表达有关,从而导致不同细胞类型的感染水平相同。我们的研究结果将人类气道中的 IAV 向性重新定义为一个阶段性解决的过程,由顶端聚糖、病毒结合、聚合酶辅因子兼容性和细胞类型特异性复制能力之间的相互作用形成,超越了以受体为中心的模型。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解禽类和人类甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 如何感染人类气道中的不同细胞类型,解决有关受体特异性、聚合酶适应性和气道向性等尚未解决的问题。
设计与对象
该研究涉及研究人类气道上皮细胞中的禽类和人类 IAV,重点关注纤毛细胞和分泌细胞。
核心方法
该研究利用人类气道上皮细胞(包括纤毛细胞和分泌细胞)来评估 IAV 结合和复制。技术包括受体分析、病毒 RNA 和蛋白质定量以及 ANP32 蛋白质相容性评估。
主要结果
由于顶端 α2,3 连接的唾液酸水平较高,禽类 IAV 优先与纤毛细胞结合,而人类 IAV 在分泌细胞中表现出较高的病毒 RNA 和蛋白质水平,这与它们与人类 ANP32 蛋白的相容性和适度较高的 ANP32A/B 表达有关。
解释与创新
这些发现将人类气道中的 IAV 趋向性重新定义为一个阶段性解决的过程,由顶端聚糖、病毒结合、聚合酶辅因子兼容性和细胞类型特异性复制能力之间的相互作用形成,超越了以受体为中心的模型。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了对人类呼吸道中 IAV 感染动态的见解,可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息,特别是了解不同细胞类型如何影响病毒传播和感染水平。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到体外模型使用的限制,可能无法完全代表体内条件。研究结果表明对 IAV 趋向性有细致入微的了解,但需要进一步的体内验证。

Opposing cell-type preferences for binding and replication shape influenza A virus infection in human airways.

Authors: von Kempis J, Pohl MO, Gaggioli E, Niklaus C, Trkola A, Boons GJ, de Vries RP, Stertz S
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A viruses (IAVs) pose a threat to human health. Although receptor specificity, polymerase adaptation, and airway tropism have been studied extensively, it remains unresolved how they combine to shape tropism in airway epithelia. Here, we show that avian IAVs infect ciliated and secretory cells but preferentially bind to ciliated cells because of higher apical α2,3-linked sialic acid levels. As replication levels are comparable between cell types, this translates into overall preference for ciliated cells. Human IAVs also preferentially bind to ciliated cells despite comparable α2,6-linked sialic acid abundance. Instead, they show higher viral RNA and protein levels in secretory cells, linked to their compatibility with human ANP32 proteins and modestly higher ANP32A/B expression in this cell type, resulting in equal infection levels across cell types. Our findings redefine IAV tropism in the human airway as a stage-resolved process shaped by the interplay between apical glycans, viral binding, polymerase cofactor compatibility, and cell-type-specific replication capacity, moving beyond receptor-centric models.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how avian and human influenza A viruses (IAVs) infect different cell types in the human airway, addressing unresolved questions about receptor specificity, polymerase adaptation, and airway tropism.
Design and population
The research involved studying avian and human IAVs in human airway epithelial cells, focusing on ciliated and secretory cells.
Core methods
The study utilized human airway epithelial cells, including ciliated and secretory cells, to assess IAV binding and replication. Techniques included receptor analysis, viral RNA and protein quantification, and assessment of ANP32 protein compatibility.
Main results
Avian IAVs preferentially bind to ciliated cells due to higher apical α2,3-linked sialic acid levels, while human IAVs show higher viral RNA and protein levels in secretory cells, linked to their compatibility with human ANP32 proteins and modestly higher ANP32A/B expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings redefine IAV tropism in the human airway as a stage-resolved process shaped by the interplay between apical glycans, viral binding, polymerase cofactor compatibility, and cell-type-specific replication capacity, moving beyond receptor-centric models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into IAV infection dynamics in the human airway, which can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies, particularly for understanding how different cell types influence viral spread and infection levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The findings suggest a nuanced understanding of IAV tropism but require further validation in vivo.

免疫功能正常患者诺卡氏菌与甲型流感病毒合并感染所致肺部感染

Pulmonary infection caused by Nocardia cyriacigeorgica and influenza A virus coinfection in an immunocompetent patient
作者： Zhengfan Yu, Yanmei Zhang, Lijie Qiu, Leqiang Wang
摘要中文翻译基本原理:肺诺卡氏菌病(PN)是由诺卡氏菌引起的机会性感染。其特点是临床症状和影像学表现缺乏特异性。大多数病例发生在免疫系统受损或患有潜在肺部疾病的个体中。相比之下,我们的患者没有出现任何这些危险因素。患者担忧:一名 60 岁免疫功能正常的男性入院,有 5 个月的反复咳嗽和咳痰病史。他的呼吸道症状恶化,入院前 9 天出现发烧症状。诊断:痰培养、支气管肺泡灌洗液二代靶向测序和培养中检出诺卡氏菌,核酸检测检出甲型流感病毒。干预措施:在住院期间,除了采用帕拉米韦 抗病毒药物 治疗外,还给予甲氧苄啶-磺胺甲恶唑 (TMP-SMX)、依替米星和美罗培南的联合抗生素治疗方案。出院后,继续口服 TMP-SMX 作为维持治疗。结果:对患者的定期随访显示,随后的胸部计算机断层扫描(CT)扫描显示病变范围逐渐缩小​​,患者表现出良好的临床反应和放射学改善。经验教训:该病例凸显了 PN 诊断的复杂性。放射学表现多样,常见包括肺实变、结节、空洞等,难以与其他疾病鉴别。早期、精准的诊断和及时的联合治疗对于重症诺卡氏菌肺部感染至关重要。

问题与背景
其特点是临床症状和影像学表现缺乏特异性。
设计与对象
患者担忧:一名 60 岁免疫功能正常的男性入院,有 5 个月的反复咳嗽和咳痰病史。
核心方法
诊断:痰培养、支气管肺泡灌洗液二代靶向测序和培养中检出诺卡氏菌,核酸检测检出甲型流感病毒。
主要结果

局限与证据强度
放射学表现多样,常见包括肺实变、结节、空洞等,难以与其他疾病鉴别。

Pulmonary infection caused by Nocardia cyriacigeorgica and influenza A virus coinfection in an immunocompetent patient

Authors: Zhengfan Yu, Yanmei Zhang, Lijie Qiu, Leqiang Wang
Original abstract (English)
Rationale: Pulmonary nocardiosis (PN) is an opportunistic infection caused by Nocardia species. It is characterized by a lack of specificity in both clinical symptoms and imaging findings. Most cases occur in individuals with compromised immune systems or underlying pulmonary diseases. In contrast, our patient did not present with any of these risk factors. Patient concerns: A 60-year-old immunocompetent male was admitted with a 5-month history of recurrent cough and expectoration. His respiratory symptoms worsened, and he developed a fever 9 days prior to hospitalization. Diagnoses: Nocardia cyriacigeorgica was identified in the sputum culture, as well as in the targeted next-generation sequencing and culture of the bronchoalveolar lavage fluid, while influenza A virus was detected by nucleic acid detection. Interventions: Along with antiviral therapy with peramivir, a combination antibiotic regimen of trimethoprim-sulfamethoxazole (TMP-SMX), etimicin, and meropenem was administered during hospitalization. Following discharge, oral TMP-SMX was continued as maintenance therapy. Outcomes: Regular follow-ups with the patient revealed that subsequent chest computed tomography (CT) scans showed a gradual reduction in the extent of the lesions, and the patient demonstrated a good clinical response and radiological improvement. Lessons: This case highlights the diagnostic complexity of PN. The radiological manifestations are diverse, commonly including pulmonary consolidation, nodules, and cavities, making it difficult to differentiate from other diseases. Early, precise diagnosis and prompt combined therapy are of vital importance in severe Nocardia pulmonary infection.

It is characterized by a lack of specificity in both clinical symptoms and imaging findings.
Design and population

Core methods
Diagnoses: Nocardia cyriacigeorgica was identified in the sputum culture, as well as in the targeted next-generation sequencing and culture of the bronchoalveolar lavage fluid, while influenza A virus was detected by nucleic acid detection.

使用甲型流感病毒片段 7 作为模型系统研究流感病毒剪接的进化可塑性

Investigating the evolutionary plasticity of influenza virus splicing using influenza A virus segment 7 as a model system
作者： Shu Zhou, Colin P Sharp, Eleanor R Gaunt, Rute Maria Pinto, Paul Digard
摘要中文翻译流感病毒 A、C 和 D(分别为 IAV、ICV、IDV)利用 mRNA 剪接从其基质基因片段产生两种蛋白质;基质 (M1) 蛋白和离子通道 (M2)。在IAV中,M1和M2开放阅读框(ORF)部分重叠,并且M1基因中的剪接供体(SD)和剪接受体(SA)位点用于产生具有与M1共享的N端序列的M2多肽。在 ICV 和 IDV 中,M1 和 M2 ORF 连接在一起,M1 编码区下游的剪接位点用于从剪接的 mRNA 产生基质蛋白。为了探究这些不同的剪接策略是如何进化的,我们使用反向遗传学来测试 IAV SD 和 SA 位点的进化灵活性。我们发现相对少量的 突变 足以激活微型复制子系统中的各种替代 SD 和 SA 位点,其中一些导致产生具有变异胞外域的离子通道亚型,以及产生 M1 变体的离子通道亚型。此外,一些剪接模式改变的病毒是可行的,其中包括五种 SD 和 SA 位点都改变的病毒。尽管 M1 中有多达 3 个非同义变化以及 M2 中有 34 个氨基酸插入,但其中 3 种“重新连接”的病毒仅产生了适度的适应性惩罚。这些结果表明,IAV 片段 7 剪接策略在进化上具有灵活性,并且对 IAV、ICV 和 IDV 矩阵片段如何从共同祖先多样化具有影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究流感病毒剪接的进化可塑性,特别关注不同的剪接策略如何在甲型、乙型和丙型流感病毒中进化。
设计与对象
该研究使用反向遗传学来测试IAV SD和SA位点的进化灵活性,采用微型复制子系统来检查突变对剪接模式的影响。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学将 突变 引入 IAV 片段 7 的 SD 和 SA 位点,并评估了微型复制子系统中由此产生的剪接模式和病毒活力。
主要结果
相对少量的 突变 足以激活替代的 SD 和 SA 位点,导致产生具有变体胞外域和 M1 变体的离子通道亚型。几种剪接模式改变的病毒是可行的,其中包括五种 SD 和 SA 位点均发生改变的病毒。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,IAV 片段 7 剪接策略在进化上是灵活的,这为了解 IAV、ICV 和 IDV 矩阵片段如何从共同祖先中多样化提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于理解流感病毒的进化机制具有重要意义,这可以为 监测、诊断 和 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于微型复制子系统,可能无法完全代表整个病毒行为的复杂性。证据是中等的,因为这些发现是基于体外实验而不是体内验证。

Investigating the evolutionary plasticity of influenza virus splicing using influenza A virus segment 7 as a model system

Authors: Shu Zhou, Colin P Sharp, Eleanor R Gaunt, Rute Maria Pinto, Paul Digard
Original abstract (English)
Influenza viruses A, C and D (IAV, ICV, IDV respectively) utilise mRNA splicing to produce two proteins from their matrix gene segment; the matrix (M1) protein and an ion channel (M2). In IAV, the M1 and M2 open reading frames (ORFs) partially overlap and splice donor (SD) and splice acceptor (SA) sites in the M1 gene are used to produce an M2 polypeptide with an N-terminal sequence shared with M1. In ICV and IDV, the M1 and M2 ORFs are concatenated and splice sites downstream of the M1 coding region are used to produce a matrix protein from the spliced mRNA. To probe how these different splicing strategies might have evolved, we used reverse genetics to test the evolutionary flexibility of IAV SD and SA sites. We found that relatively small numbers of mutations were sufficient to activate a variety of alternative SD and SA sites in a mini-replicon system, several of which led to the production of ion channel isoforms with variant ectodomains, as well as one that produced an M1 variant. Furthermore, several of the viruses with altered splicing patterns were viable, including five with both SD and SA sites changed. Three of these 'rewired' viruses had only modest fitness penalties, despite up to 3 nonsynonymous changes in M1 and a 34 amino acid insertion in M2. These results suggest that IAV segment 7 splicing strategies are evolutionarily flexible and have implications for how IAV, ICV and IDV matrix segments might have diversified from a common ancestor.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the evolutionary plasticity of influenza virus splicing, particularly focusing on how different splicing strategies might have evolved across influenza A, B, and C viruses.
Design and population
The study used reverse genetics to test the evolutionary flexibility of IAV SD and SA sites, employing a mini-replicon system to examine the effects of mutations on splicing patterns.
Core methods
The researchers utilized reverse genetics to introduce mutations into the SD and SA sites of the IAV segment 7 and assessed the resulting splicing patterns and viral viability in a mini-replicon system.
Main results
Relatively small numbers of mutations were sufficient to activate alternative SD and SA sites, leading to the production of ion channel isoforms with variant ectodomains and an M1 variant. Several viruses with altered splicing patterns were viable, including five with both SD and SA sites changed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that IAV segment 7 splicing strategies are evolutionarily flexible, providing insights into how IAV, ICV, and IDV matrix segments might have diversified from a common ancestor.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have implications for understanding the evolutionary mechanisms of influenza viruses, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and the development of antiviral strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mini-replicon system, which may not fully represent the complexity of whole-virus behavior. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on in vitro experiments and not in vivo validation.

TLR7 缺陷揭示了严重甲型流感病毒感染期间特定的呼吸系统 免疫应答

TLR7 Deficiency Reveals Compartment-Specific Respiratory–Systemic Immune Responses During Severe Influenza A Virus Infection
作者： Mark A. Miles, Stella Liong, John J. O’Leary, Doug A. Brooks, Stavros Selemidis
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 感染可从局部呼吸道疾病发展为以肺部炎症、全身细胞因子血症、急性期反应和多器官病理为特征的严重疾病。Toll样受体7(TLR7)是病毒单链RNA的关键传感器,也是抗病毒药物免疫的重要组成部分;然而,在严重 IAV 感染期间,其在将免疫力从呼吸道扩展到外周区室中的作用仍不完全清楚。在这里,我们研究了 IAV 感染期间 TLR7 与呼吸和外周区室的先天、细胞和体液 免疫应答 之间的关系。使用感染高致病性 PR8 H1N1 毒株的 TLR7 缺陷小鼠,我们分析了鼻组织、支气管肺泡灌洗液、肺、血液和脾脏中的 免疫应答,以及细胞因子的产生、C 反应蛋白水平、T 细胞激活和抗体同种型反应。TLR7的缺失伴随着先天免疫的区室特异性改变,包括肺巨噬细胞和树突状细胞反应的差异,以及呼吸和外周区室中浆细胞样树突细胞分布的改变。还观察到病毒特异性 CD8+ T 细胞扩增减少、T 细胞产生的 IFNγ 和 TNFα 降低、循环炎症细胞因子和 C 反应蛋白减少。与此同时,体液反应的改变包括粘膜 IgA、全身 IgM 和 Th1 相关 IgG2a 反应的减少,以及抗体同种型分布的变化。总的来说,这些发现表明,TLR7 缺陷与 IAV 感染期间先天性和适应性 免疫应答 的广泛的、区室特异性的改变有关,这种改变延伸到呼吸道、血液和脾脏,并与疾病的严重程度相关。这些发现支持 TLR7 相关信号在塑造更广泛的呼吸系统 免疫应答 中的作用,其存在与 抗病毒药物 免疫参与和严重 IAV 感染期间全身炎症的严重程度有关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查严重甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染期间TLR7在将免疫力从呼吸道扩展到外周区室中的作用,解决IAV感染期间TLR7和免疫应答在呼吸道和外周区室之间不完全了解的关系。
设计与对象
该研究使用感染高致病性 PR8 H1N1 毒株的 TLR7 缺陷小鼠来分析鼻组织、支气管肺泡灌洗液、肺、血液和脾脏中的 免疫应答,以及细胞因子的产生、C 反应蛋白水平、T 细胞激活和抗体同种型反应。
核心方法
TLR7 缺陷型小鼠感染 PR8 H1N1 病毒株,并利用细胞因子产生、C 反应蛋白水平、T 细胞激活和抗体同种型反应,对鼻组织、支气管肺泡灌洗液、肺、血液和脾脏等不同部位的 免疫应答 进行分析。
主要结果
TLR7 缺陷与先天免疫的区室特异性改变有关,包括肺巨噬细胞和树突状细胞反应的差异,以及呼吸和外周区室中浆细胞样树突细胞分布的改变。此外,还观察到病毒特异性 CD8+ T 细胞扩增减少、T 细胞产生的 IFNγ 和 TNFα 降低、循环炎症细胞因子和 C 反应蛋白减少。体液反应还包括粘膜 IgA、全身 IgM 和 Th1 相关 IgG2a 反应减少,以及抗体同种型分布的变化。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,TLR7 缺陷与 IAV 感染期间先天性和适应性 免疫应答 的广泛的、区室特异性的改变相关,延伸到呼吸道、血液和脾脏,并与疾病的严重程度相关。这支持了 TLR7 相关信号在塑造更广泛的呼吸系统 免疫应答 中的作用,其存在与 抗病毒药物 免疫参与和严重 IAV 感染期间全身炎症的严重程度有关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在严重 IAV 感染期间,TLR7 在将免疫力从呼吸道扩展到外周区室中发挥着关键作用,这可能对理解和管理严重流感疾病具有重要意义。这可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息,特别是在严重 IAV 感染的情况下。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括使用单一病毒株和特定的小鼠模型,这可能会限制普遍性。由于使用单一病毒株并且需要在不同病毒株和人类模型中进一步验证,证据强度中等。

TLR7 Deficiency Reveals Compartment-Specific Respiratory–Systemic Immune Responses During Severe Influenza A Virus Infection

Authors: Mark A. Miles, Stella Liong, John J. O’Leary, Doug A. Brooks, Stavros Selemidis
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) infection can progress from a localized respiratory illness to severe disease characterized by pulmonary inflammation, systemic cytokinemia, acute-phase responses and multi-organ pathology. Toll-like receptor 7 (TLR7) is a key sensor of viral single-stranded RNA and an important component of antiviral immunity; however, its role in extending immunity from the respiratory tract to peripheral compartments during severe IAV infection remains incompletely understood. Here, we investigated the relationship between TLR7 and innate, cellular and humoral immune responses across respiratory and peripheral compartments during IAV infection. Using TLR7-deficient mice infected with the highly pathogenic PR8 H1N1 strain, we profiled immune responses in nasal tissue, bronchoalveolar lavage fluid, lung, blood and spleen, together with cytokine production, C-reactive protein levels, T cell activation and antibody isotype responses. Loss of TLR7 was accompanied by compartment-specific alterations in innate immunity, including differences in pulmonary macrophage and dendritic cell responses, and altered plasmacytoid dendritic cell distribution across respiratory and peripheral compartments. Reduced virus-specific CD8+ T cell expansion, lower IFNγ and TNFα production by T cells, and diminished circulating inflammatory cytokines and C-reactive protein were also observed. In parallel, altered humoral responses included reduced mucosal IgA, systemic IgM and Th1-associated IgG2a responses, together with changes in antibody isotype distribution. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that TLR7 deficiency is associated with broad, compartment-specific alterations in innate and adaptive immune responses during established IAV infection, extending across the respiratory tract, blood and spleen and correlating with disease severity. These findings support a role for TLR7-associated signaling in shaping the broader respiratory–systemic immune response, with its presence linked to both antiviral immune engagement and the magnitude of systemic inflammation during severe IAV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of TLR7 in extending immunity from the respiratory tract to peripheral compartments during severe influenza A virus (IAV) infection, addressing the incompletely understood relationship between TLR7 and immune responses across respiratory and peripheral compartments during IAV infection.
Design and population
The study used TLR7-deficient mice infected with the highly pathogenic PR8 H1N1 strain to profile immune responses in nasal tissue, bronchoalveolar lavage fluid, lung, blood, and spleen, along with cytokine production, C-reactive protein levels, T cell activation, and antibody isotype responses.
Core methods
TLR7-deficient mice were infected with the PR8 H1N1 strain, and immune responses were profiled in various compartments including nasal tissue, bronchoalveolar lavage fluid, lung, blood, and spleen, using cytokine production, C-reactive protein levels, T cell activation, and antibody isotype responses.
Main results
TLR7 deficiency was associated with compartment-specific alterations in innate immunity, including differences in pulmonary macrophage and dendritic cell responses, and altered plasmacytoid dendritic cell distribution across respiratory and peripheral compartments. Additionally, reduced virus-specific CD8+ T cell expansion, lower IFNγ and TNFα production by T cells, and diminished circulating inflammatory cytokines and C-reactive protein were observed. Humoral responses also included reduced mucosal IgA, systemic IgM, and Th1-associated IgG2a responses, with changes in antibody isotype distribution.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate that TLR7 deficiency is associated with broad, compartment-specific alterations in innate and adaptive immune responses during established IAV infection, extending across the respiratory tract, blood, and spleen and correlating with disease severity. This supports a role for TLR7-associated signaling in shaping the broader respiratory–systemic immune response, with its presence linked to both antiviral immune engagement and the magnitude of systemic inflammation during severe IAV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that TLR7 plays a critical role in extending immunity from the respiratory tract to peripheral compartments during severe IAV infection, which could have implications for understanding and managing severe influenza disease. This could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies, particularly in the context of severe IAV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the use of a single viral strain and a specific mouse model, which may limit generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the use of a single viral strain and the need for further validation in different viral strains and human models.

环状RNA MARCH3通过协调NF-κB负反馈和miR-3657/ATG7轴促进甲型流感病毒复制

Circular RNA MARCH3 promotes influenza A virus replication by coordinating NF-κB negative feedback and the miR-3657/ATG7 axis
作者： Jiayin Liu, Jiarong Song, Ling’en Yang, Mohamed Maarouf, Haori Qiu, Shasha Liu, Shile Huang, Song Wang, Ji-Long Chen
摘要中文翻译引言 环状 RNA (circRNA) 已成为多种细胞过程的重要调节因子,越来越多的证据表明某些 circRNA 通过复杂的机制调节 免疫应答。然而,它们在针对甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染的先天免疫中的作用仍然知之甚少。方法我们通过 RNA-seq、RNase R 消化测定和 RNA 稳定性测定鉴定了一种先前未表征的 circRNA,称为 circMARCH3。我们通过 RT-PCR 和实时 PCR 检查了病毒感染后多种细胞类型中 circMARCH3 的表达,研究了驱动其诱导的信号通路,并使用 circMARCH3 过表达 媒介 和 shRNA 介导的敲低进行了功能分析。此外,我们通过机制研究鉴定并验证了 circMARCH3/miR-3657/ATG7 轴。结果 circMARCH3 由膜相关的 RING-CH-Type Finger 3 (MARCH3) 基因的前体 mRNA 生成,其表达在多种细胞类型中受到 IAV 和其他几种病毒感染的强烈诱导。从机制上讲,病毒诱导的 circMARCH3 表达是由 NF-κB 信号通路驱动的。功能分析表明,circMARCH3过表达显着促进IAV复制,而circMARCH3敲低则显着抑制病毒产生。与这些发现一致的是,circMARCH3 通过减弱 NF-κB 激活和 I 型干扰素反应,充当 抗病毒药物 先天免疫的负调节因子。此外,我们还发现 circMARCH3 靶向 miR-3657,这是一种抑制 ATG7 表达的 microRNA,ATG7 是一种关键的自噬调节因子和已知的 pro-IAV 因子。机制研究表明,circMARCH3 作为竞争性内源 RNA (ceRNA),隔离 miR-3657,从而缓解其对 ATG7 表达的抑制作用,从而增强自噬并增加病毒复制。讨论 结果表明,circMARCH3 作为病毒诱导型 circRNA,通过两种互补机制促进 IAV 感染:抑制 NF-κB 介导的 抗病毒药物 先天免疫和通过 circMARCH3/miR-3657/ATG7 轴促进自噬。我们的研究结果表明,circMARCH3 通过协调 NF-κB 负反馈和 miR-3657/ATG7 轴,成为 宿主 和 IAV 之间复杂相互作用的新型调节剂。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查环状 RNA (circRNA) 在调节 免疫应答 中的作用,特别关注它们在针对甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 感染的先天免疫中的潜在参与。
设计与对象
该研究在多种细胞类型中进行,检查病毒感染后的 circMARCH3 表达。它涉及 circMARCH3 过表达和敲低实验,以评估其对 IAV 复制的影响。
核心方法
circMARCH3 通过 RNA-seq、RNase R 消化测定和 RNA 稳定性测定进行鉴定。使用 RT-PCR 和实时 PCR 分析其在各种细胞类型中的表达,并使用过表达 媒介 和 shRNA 介导的敲低研究其功能作用。
主要结果
circMARCH3 由 MARCH3 基因的前 mRNA 生成,其表达在多种细胞类型中受到 IAV 和其他几种病毒感染的强烈诱导。功能分析表明,circMARCH3过表达显着促进IAV复制,而敲低则显着抑制病毒产生。
解释与创新
结果表明,circMARCH3 通过抑制 NF-κB 介导的 抗病毒药物 先天免疫并通过 circMARCH3/miR-3657/ATG7 轴促进自噬来促进 IAV 感染。这使得 circMARCH3 成为 宿主 与病毒相互作用的新型调节因子。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 IAV 复制的分子机制的见解,并提出了旨在调节 宿主 免疫应答 流感的治疗干预的潜在目标。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注细胞培养模型且缺乏体内验证而受到限制。然而,由于使用了多种实验方法来证实 circMARCH3 在 IAV 感染中的作用,证据是强有力的。

Circular RNA MARCH3 promotes influenza A virus replication by coordinating NF-κB negative feedback and the miR-3657/ATG7 axis

Authors: Jiayin Liu, Jiarong Song, Ling’en Yang, Mohamed Maarouf, Haori Qiu, Shasha Liu, Shile Huang, Song Wang, Ji-Long Chen
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Circular RNAs (circRNAs) have emerged as important regulators of diverse cellular processes, and accumulating evidence indicates that certain circRNAs modulate immune responses through complex mechanisms. However, their roles in innate immunity against influenza A virus (IAV) infection remain poorly understood. Methods We identified a previously uncharacterized circRNA, termed circMARCH3, through RNA-seq, RNase R digestion assay, and RNA stability assay. We examined circMARCH3 expression in multiple cell types following viral infection by RT-PCR and real-time PCR, investigated the signaling pathway driving its induction, and performed functional analyses using circMARCH3 overexpression vectors and shRNAs-mediated knockdown. Furthermore, we identified and validated the circMARCH3/miR-3657/ATG7 axis through mechanistic studies. Results circMARCH3 is generated from the pre-mRNA of the membrane-associated RING-CH-Type Finger 3 (MARCH3) gene, and its expression was robustly induced by IAV and several other viral infections in multiple cell types. Mechanistically, virus-induced circMARCH3 expression was driven by the NF-κB signaling pathway. Functional analyses demonstrated that circMARCH3 overexpression significantly promoted IAV replication, whereas circMARCH3 knockdown markedly inhibited viral production. Consistent with these findings, circMARCH3 functioned as a negative regulator of antiviral innate immunity by attenuating NF-κB activation and type I interferon responses. Furthermore, we identified that circMARCH3 targeted miR-3657, a microRNA that suppresses the expression of ATG7, a key autophagy regulator and known pro-IAV factor. Mechanistic studies revealed that circMARCH3 acted as a competitive endogenous RNA (ceRNA), sequestering miR-3657 and thereby relieving its repressive effect on ATG7 expression, resulting in enhanced autophagy and increased viral replication. Discussion The results demonstrate circMARCH3 as a virus-inducible circRNA that promotes IAV infection through two complementary mechanisms: dampening NF-κB-mediated antiviral innate immunity and facilitating autophagy via the circMARCH3/miR-3657/ATG7 axis. Our findings establish circMARCH3 as a novel regulator of the complex interaction between host and IAV by coordinating NF-κB negative feedback and the miR-3657/ATG7 axis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of circular RNAs (circRNAs) in modulating immune responses, particularly focusing on their potential involvement in innate immunity against influenza A virus (IAV) infection.
Design and population
The study was conducted in multiple cell types, examining circMARCH3 expression following viral infection. It involved both circMARCH3 overexpression and knockdown experiments to assess its impact on IAV replication.
Core methods
circMARCH3 was identified through RNA-seq, RNase R digestion assay, and RNA stability assay. Its expression was analyzed in various cell types using RT-PCR and real-time PCR, and its functional role was investigated using overexpression vectors and shRNAs-mediated knockdown.
Main results
circMARCH3 is generated from the pre-mRNA of the MARCH3 gene and its expression was robustly induced by IAV and several other viral infections in multiple cell types. Functional analyses showed that circMARCH3 overexpression significantly promoted IAV replication, whereas knockdown markedly inhibited viral production.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that circMARCH3 promotes IAV infection by dampening NF-κB-mediated antiviral innate immunity and facilitating autophagy through the circMARCH3/miR-3657/ATG7 axis. This establishes circMARCH3 as a novel regulator of host-virus interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of IAV replication and suggest potential targets for therapeutic interventions aimed at modulating host immune responses to influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on cell culture models and the absence of in vivo validation. However, the evidence is strong due to the use of multiple experimental approaches to confirm circMARCH3's role in IAV infection.

甲型H3N2流感(H3N2) ของกลมคนพกพงในสถานสงเคราะหแหงหนง จงหวดศรสะเกษประเทศไทยระหวางเดอนมถายน -第 2568 章

การสอบสวนการระบาดโรคไขหวดใหญ สายพนธ A (H3N2) ของกลมคนพกพงใน สถานสงเคราะหแหงหนง จงหวดศรสะเกษ ประเทศไทย ระหวางเดอนมถนายน - กรกฎาคม 2568
作者： Chainan Bootkan, Chirawan Khamsri, Sattaya Hirunprasertkul, Kaittisak Poungploy, Sirasa Karobsuwan, Ratcharin Khammarat, Suphanan Chantakorn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025年6月至7月,对四色菊府Khukhan区社会福利中心的居民和工作人员进行了甲型H3N2流感暴发调查。目的是确认诊断和流行病学的分布特征,并找出潜在原因和风险因素,以指导有效的防控措施。
设计与对象
采用描述性 流行病学 和回顾性 队列研究 研究。使用案例调查表积极寻找和收集数据和活跃案例。该研究包括 317 名居民和工作人员,其中 205 人符合病例定义。
核心方法
获取鼻咽拭子进行实验室确认,并进行环境评估。使用多变量分析来确定与感染相关的重要危险因素。
主要结果
Out of 317 residents and staff, 205 met the case definition, resulting in an attack rate of 64.67%. These cases comprised 13 confirmed, 10 probable, and 182 suspected cases. The five most common clinical manifestations were cough (60.49%), fever (55.12%), rhinorrhea (43.41%), headache (37.07%), and sore throat (28.78%). Six deaths were reported, representing a mortality rate of 1.81%. Multivariate analysis revealed that significant risk factors associated with the infection included sleeping in close proximity (ORadj=1.92, 95% CI: 1.01-3.63), utilizing a communal bathtub on wet floors (ORadj=17.55, 95% CI: 8.94-34.46), sharing drinking cups (ORadj=3.42, 95% CI: 1.51-7.75), and dining in groups (ORadj=3.12, 95% CI: 1.46-6.69).
解释与创新
暴发 是由甲型流感 (H3N2) 病毒引起的。经过采取严格的控制措施,包括患者隔离、表面清洁和消毒、严格执行佩戴口罩和手部卫生、对避难所用水进行氯化处理和疫苗接种,该暴发被成功遏制和终止。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为类似环境中的有针对性的预防策略提供参考,特别强调卫生习惯和环境控制在减少 传播 方面的重要性。研究结果还强调需要改进社会福利中心的 监测 和快速反应机制。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的回忆偏差而受到限制。然而,由于详细的病例调查和风险因素的多变量分析,证据是强有力的。

การสอบสวนการระบาดโรคไขหวดใหญ สายพนธ A (H3N2) ของกลมคนพกพงใน สถานสงเคราะหแหงหนง จงหวดศรสะเกษ ประเทศไทย ระหวางเดอนมถนายน - กรกฎาคม 2568

Authors: Chainan Bootkan, Chirawan Khamsri, Sattaya Hirunprasertkul, Kaittisak Poungploy, Sirasa Karobsuwan, Ratcharin Khammarat, Suphanan Chantakorn
Original abstract (English)
An influenza A (H3N2) outbreak investigation was conducted among residents and staff at a social welfare center in Khukhan District, Sisaket Province, between June and July 2025. The objectives were to confirm the diagnosis and epidemiology, the distribution characteristics, and to identify potential causes and risk factors to guide effective prevention and control measures. Descriptive epidemiology and a retrospective cohort study were utilized. Data and active cases were actively sought and collected using case investigation forms. Nasopharyngeal swabs were obtained for laboratory confirmation, and environmental assessments were performed. Results: Out of 317 residents and staff, 205 met the case definition, resulting in an attack rate of 64.67%. These cases comprised 13 confirmed, 10 probable, and 182 suspected cases. The majority were male (136 cases) and female (36 cases), with a male-to-female ratio of 1.97:1. The median age was 49 years (range: 20–78 years). The five most common clinical manifestations were cough (60.49%), fever (55.12%), rhinorrhea (43.41%), headache (37.07%), and sore throat (28.78%). Six deaths were reported, representing a mortality rate of 1.81%. Multivariate analysis revealed that significant risk factors associated with the infection included sleeping in close proximity (ORadj=1.92, 95% CI: 1.01-3.63), utilizing a communal bathtub on wet floors (ORadj=17.55, 95% CI: 8.94-34.46), sharing drinking cups (ORadj=3.42, 95% CI: 1.51-7.75), and dining in groups (ORadj=3.12, 95% CI: 1.46-6.69). This outbreak was caused by Influenza A (H3N2) virus. The outbreak was successfully contained and terminated following the implementation of strict control measures, including patient isolation, surface cleaning and disinfection, strict enforcement of mask-wearing and hand hygiene, chlorination of water for shelter use, and vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
An influenza A (H3N2) outbreak investigation was conducted among residents and staff at a social welfare center in Khukhan District, Sisaket Province, between June and July 2025. The objectives were to confirm the diagnosis and epidemiology, the distribution characteristics, and to identify potential causes and risk factors to guide effective prevention and control measures.
Design and population
Descriptive epidemiology and a retrospective cohort study were utilized. Data and active cases were actively sought and collected using case investigation forms. The study included 317 residents and staff, with 205 meeting the case definition.
Core methods
Nasopharyngeal swabs were obtained for laboratory confirmation, and environmental assessments were performed. Multivariate analysis was used to identify significant risk factors associated with the infection.
Main results
Out of 317 residents and staff, 205 met the case definition, resulting in an attack rate of 64.67%. These cases comprised 13 confirmed, 10 probable, and 182 suspected cases. The five most common clinical manifestations were cough (60.49%), fever (55.12%), rhinorrhea (43.41%), headache (37.07%), and sore throat (28.78%). Six deaths were reported, representing a mortality rate of 1.81%. Multivariate analysis revealed that significant risk factors associated with the infection included sleeping in close proximity (ORadj=1.92, 95% CI: 1.01-3.63), utilizing a communal bathtub on wet floors (ORadj=17.55, 95% CI: 8.94-34.46), sharing drinking cups (ORadj=3.42, 95% CI: 1.51-7.75), and dining in groups (ORadj=3.12, 95% CI: 1.46-6.69).
Interpretation and novelty
The outbreak was caused by Influenza A (H3N2) virus. This outbreak was successfully contained and terminated following the implementation of strict control measures, including patient isolation, surface cleaning and disinfection, strict enforcement of mask-wearing and hand hygiene, chlorination of water for shelter use, and vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted prevention strategies in similar settings, particularly emphasizing the importance of hygiene practices and environmental controls in reducing transmission. The results also highlight the need for improved surveillance and rapid response mechanisms in social welfare centers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for recall bias. However, the evidence is strong due to the detailed case investigation and multivariate analysis of risk factors.

幼稚雪貂和免疫前雪貂 (Mustela putorius Furo) 模型中流感疾病严重程度的血液学和生化预后指标

Hematologic and biochemical prognostic indicators of influenza disease severity in naïve and preimmune ferret (Mustela putorius furo) models
作者： Afton Barkes, Troy J. Kieran, Gregory Daggett, Taronna R. Maines, Sherrie M. Jean, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza
摘要中文翻译摘要 雪貂 ( Mustela putorius furo ) 被广泛用于评估流感病毒的致病性和大流行风险,但这些评估中通常不包括血液学和生化参数。在这项研究中,我们对鼻内接种不同流感病毒株后的免疫学幼稚雪貂和免疫前雪貂的全血细胞计数和血清化学特征进行了比较分析。季节性 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒以及毒力较低的 A(H5N1) 病毒会导致幼雪貂出现轻微的非致命性疾病,并伴有有限或短暂的血液检查变化。相比之下,接受高毒力 A(H5N1) 病毒攻击的幼稚雪貂表现出上呼吸道以外的病毒检测,疾病进展迅速,伴随着明显的代谢和生化破坏,需要人道安乐死。预先存在的免疫力在受到这种病毒攻击后改变了疾病进展,与 甲型H3N2流感病毒 免疫前雪貂相比,甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 免疫前雪貂表现出病毒复制减少、疾病不那么严重以及生化破坏不那么明显。总体而言,两种雪貂模型在急性感染期间淋巴细胞减少都很常见。然而,它没有区分非致命疾病和致命疾病。相反,恶化的生化变化,包括高血糖、丙氨酸氨基转移酶、总胆红素、淀粉酶、球蛋白和电解质紊乱增加,在患有严重甲型H5N1疾病的雪貂中最为明显。这些发现支持使用连续血液学和血清化学分析作为评估疾病严重程度、免疫介导的保护以及幼稚和免疫前雪貂模型中的人道终点决策的补充终点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估血液学和生化参数在评估雪貂流感疾病严重程度中的作用,满足流感研究中额外终点的需求。
设计与对象
该研究比较了幼稚雪貂和免疫前雪貂鼻内接种不同流感病毒株后的全血细胞计数和血清化学特征,研究于未指定的日期在未指定的中心进行。
核心方法
该研究涉及给雪貂接种 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 A(H5N1) 病毒,并分析血液学和生化参数。
主要结果
感染 A(H5N1) 会导致严重的疾病,并伴有明显的代谢和生化破坏,而 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 则导致轻度、非致命性疾病,且血液学和生化变化有限。
解释与创新
该研究强调了连续血液学和血清化学分析在评估雪貂疾病严重程度和免疫介导保护方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用血液学和生化参数作为雪貂模型中评估流感疾病严重程度和人道终点决策的补充终点。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用的样本量较小以及缺乏与人类流感的直接比较,这可能会限制普遍性。

Hematologic and biochemical prognostic indicators of influenza disease severity in naïve and preimmune ferret (Mustela putorius furo) models

Authors: Afton Barkes, Troy J. Kieran, Gregory Daggett, Taronna R. Maines, Sherrie M. Jean, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Ferrets ( Mustela putorius furo ) are widely used to evaluate influenza virus pathogenicity and pandemic risk, but hematologic and biochemical parameters are not routinely included in these assessments. In this study, we performed a comparative analysis of complete blood count and serum chemistry profiles in immunologically naïve and preimmune ferrets following intranasal inoculation with different influenza virus strains. Seasonal A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H3N2) viruses and a less virulent A(H5N1) virus caused mild, nonfatal disease with limited or transient bloodwork changes in naïve ferrets. In contrast, naïve ferrets challenged with a highly virulent A(H5N1) virus showed virus detection beyond the upper respiratory tract, rapid disease progression accompanied by pronounced metabolic and biochemical disruption, necessitating humane euthanasia. Pre-existing immunity altered disease progression following challenge with this virus, with A(H1N1)pdm09-preimmune ferrets displaying reduced viral replication, less severe disease, and less pronounced biochemical disruption compared to A(H3N2)-preimmune ferrets. Overall, lymphopenia was common during acute infection in both ferret models; however, it did not distinguish nonfatal from fatal disease. Conversely, worsening biochemical changes, including hyperglycemia, increased alanine aminotransferase, total bilirubin, amylase, globulin, and electrolyte disturbances, were most evident in ferrets with severe A(H5N1) disease. These findings support the use of serial hematologic and serum chemistry analyses as complementary endpoints for evaluating disease severity, immune-mediated protection, and humane endpoint decisions in naïve and preimmune ferret models.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the role of hematologic and biochemical parameters in assessing influenza disease severity in ferrets, addressing the need for additional endpoints in influenza research.
Design and population
The study compared complete blood count and serum chemistry profiles in naïve and preimmune ferrets following intranasal inoculation with different influenza virus strains, conducted over unspecified dates at unspecified centres.
Core methods
The study involved inoculating ferrets with A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and A(H5N1) viruses and analyzing hematologic and biochemical parameters.
Main results
Infection with A(H5N1) caused severe disease with pronounced metabolic and biochemical disruption, while A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H3N2) caused mild, nonfatal disease with limited hematologic and biochemical changes.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted the importance of serial hematologic and serum chemistry analyses in evaluating disease severity and immune-mediated protection in ferrets.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of hematologic and biochemical parameters as complementary endpoints for assessing influenza disease severity and humane endpoint decisions in ferret models.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a small sample size and the absence of a direct comparison with human influenza, which may limit generalizability.

评估系统发育重建错误对分段病毒遗传重配推断的影响。

Assessing the impact of phylogenetic reconstruction error on genetic reassortment inference in segmented viruses.
作者： Hugo Gildardo Castelán-Sánchez, Art F. Y. Poon
摘要中文翻译重组是RNA病毒进化中的一个重要过程。对于具有分段 基因组 的病毒,可以通过整个基因组片段的重配来发生重组。这一过程在具有新的基因组组合(即抗原转变)的流感病毒的适应和传播中发挥了重要作用。重配通常是通过从不同基因组片段重建的树的拓扑之间的差异来识别的,例如子树修剪和重新移植(SPR)重排。然而,这种系统发育的不一致也可能是由于信息不足导致树重建错误造成的。本研究的目的是评估系统发育重建误差对从禽类 宿主 中推断甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 基因组 重配事件的贡献。我们整理了 11,795 个完整禽类 H5Nx IAV 基因组 的数据集,并采样了具有不同预期重建误差水平的 HA 序列子集。接下来,我们沿着从这些 HA 样本重建的树模拟分子 进化,改变 进化 的比率和序列长度以对应于其他基因组片段。由于这些模拟没有重配,因此从所得树中识别出的任何系统发育不一致必定是由重建错误引起的。我们发现,随机样本的模拟产生的 SPR 大约是真实数据的 32%。当模拟来自 HA 序列遗传差异最大子集的树木时,假阳性率降低至 4%。通过用 SPR 重新排列修改模拟树,我们观察到假阴性率为 8.5%,并且随着每棵树的 SPR 数量的增加而增加。总的来说,我们发现重配可能因系统发育不一致而被大大高估,这强调了在分析 IAV 重配数据集时需要考虑系统发育的不确定性和抽样设计。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重组是RNA病毒进化中的一个重要过程。对于具有分段 基因组 的病毒,可以通过整个基因组片段的重配来发生重组。
设计与对象
本研究的目的是评估系统发育重建误差对从禽类 宿主 中推断甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 基因组 重配事件的贡献。我们整理了 11,795 个完整禽类 H5Nx IAV 基因组 的数据集,并采样了具有不同预期重建误差水平的 HA 序列子集。
核心方法
接下来,我们沿着从这些 HA 样本重建的树模拟分子 进化,改变 进化 的比率和序列长度以对应于其他基因组片段。由于这些模拟没有重配,因此从所得树中识别出的任何系统发育不一致必定是由重建错误引起的。
主要结果
我们发现,随机样本的模拟产生的 SPR 大约是真实数据的 32%。当模拟来自 HA 序列遗传差异最大子集的树木时,假阳性率降低至 4%。通过用 SPR 重新排列修改模拟树,我们观察到假阴性率为 8.5%,并且随着每棵树的 SPR 数量的增加而增加。
解释与创新
总的来说,我们发现重配可能因系统发育不一致而被大大高估,这强调了在分析 IAV 重配数据集时需要考虑系统发育的不确定性和抽样设计。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在 IAV 重配数据集分析中考虑系统发育不确定性和抽样设计的重要性,这可能对理解病毒 进化 和改进 监测 策略产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于模拟数据,不包括现实世界的验证,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据是温和的,因为它是基于模拟而不是直接观察。

Assessing the impact of phylogenetic reconstruction error on genetic reassortment inference in segmented viruses.

Authors: Hugo Gildardo Castelán-Sánchez, Art F. Y. Poon
Original abstract (English)
Recombination is an important process in the evolution of RNA viruses. For viruses with segmented genomes, recombination can occur by reassortment of entire genomic segments. This process has played a significant role in the adaptation and spread of influenza viruses with novel genomic combinations, i.e., antigenic shifts. Reassortment is usually identified by discrepancies between the topologies of trees reconstructed from different genomic segments, e.g., subtree prune and regraft (SPR) rearrangements. However, this phylogenetic discordance can also result from errors in tree reconstruction due to insufficient information. The objective of this study was to evaluate the contribution of phylogenetic reconstruction error to the inference of reassortment events from influenza A virus (IAV) genomes from avian hosts. We curated a dataset of 11,795 complete avian H5Nx IAV genomes and sampled subsets of HA sequences with different expected levels of reconstruction error. Next, we simulated molecular evolution along the trees reconstructed from these HA samples, varying rates of evolution and sequence lengths to correspond to the other genomic segments. Because these simulations had no reassortment, any phylogenetic discordance identified from the resulting trees must be caused by reconstruction error. We found that simulations from random samples yielded about 32% as many SPRs as obtained from real data. This false positive rate was reduced to 4% when simulating trees from the most genetically divergent subset of HA sequences. By modifying the simulated trees with SPR rearrangements, we observed a false negative rate of 8.5%, which increased with the number of SPRs per tree. Overall, we found that reassortment can be substantially overestimated from phylogenetic discordance, underscoring the need to account for phylogenetic uncertainty and sampling design when analyzing IAV datasets for reassortment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Recombination is an important process in the evolution of RNA viruses. For viruses with segmented genomes, recombination can occur by reassortment of entire genomic segments.
Design and population
The objective of this study was to evaluate the contribution of phylogenetic reconstruction error to the inference of reassortment events from influenza A virus (IAV) genomes from avian hosts. We curated a dataset of 11,795 complete avian H5Nx IAV genomes and sampled subsets of HA sequences with different expected levels of reconstruction error.
Core methods
Next, we simulated molecular evolution along the trees reconstructed from these HA samples, varying rates of evolution and sequence lengths to correspond to the other genomic segments. Because these simulations had no reassortment, any phylogenetic discordance identified from the resulting trees must be caused by reconstruction error.
Main results
We found that simulations from random samples yielded about 32% as many SPRs as obtained from real data. This false positive rate was reduced to 4% when simulating trees from the most genetically divergent subset of HA sequences. By modifying the simulated trees with SPR rearrangements, we observed a false negative rate of 8.5%, which increased with the number of SPRs per tree.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, we found that reassortment can be substantially overestimated from phylogenetic discordance, underscoring the need to account for phylogenetic uncertainty and sampling design when analyzing IAV datasets for reassortment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of considering phylogenetic uncertainty and sampling design in the analysis of IAV datasets for reassortment, which can have implications for understanding viral evolution and improving surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to simulated data and does not include real-world validation, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate as it is based on simulations and not direct observations.
