数学 AI 预测 2026--2027 年美国季节 疫苗 对 H1N1 和 H3N2 流感的有效性分别为 40% 和 57%

Mathematical AI Predicts 40% and 57% Influenza Vaccine Effectiveness Against H1N1 and H3N2, Respectively, for the 2026--2027 U.S. Season
作者： Xiang Xiang Wang, Guo-Wei Wei, Wang, X. X.
摘要中文翻译背景:在过去二十年中,临床流感 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 往往较低,这凸显了季前 VE 预测的必要性。及时的预测可以帮助卫生当局规划预防策略并分配医疗资源。然而,临床流感 VE 的季前预测仍然相对不常见。方法:我们从 GISAID 获得了 2010--2011 年至 2024--2025 年每个流感季节前 8 月至 9 月采集的美国样本的血凝素 (HA) 序列。经过质量筛选后,我们使用基于 HA 蛋白氨基酸模式的两个数学距离,将每种病毒与同一季节、亚型或谱系的相应 疫苗 目标毒株进行比较。我们计算了每个季节和亚型或谱系的平均距离,并将它们与已发表的临床 VE 估计值配对,在 13 个季节中产生了 26 个观察值。使用数学人工智能方法,我们针对每个距离拟合了 VE 线性回归模型,并通过使用早期季节的数据预测后期季节来评估其预测性能,并以每个亚型或谱系的历史平均 VE 作为参考。然后,我们对 2026 年 8 月至 9 月收集的 87 个 HA 序列应用相同的方法,以预测 2026 年至 2027 年季节的 VE。结果:我们对美国即将到来的 2026--2027 年流感 VE 进行了特定成分的预测。这两个数学距离均与历史 VE 负相关,表明流行病毒和 疫苗 毒株之间较大的差异与较低的 VE 相关。拓扑模型预测 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的 VE 分别为 40.3%、56.9% 和 47.1%,而几何模型预测相应的 VE 值为 34.0%、56.8% 和 51.1%。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在过去二十年中,临床流感 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 往往较低,这凸显了季前 VE 预测的必要性。
设计与对象
及时的预测可以帮助卫生当局规划预防策略并分配医疗资源。
核心方法
我们从 GISAID 获得了 2010--2011 年至 2024--2025 年每个流感季节前 8 月至 9 月采集的美国样本的血凝素 (HA) 序列。经过质量筛选后,我们使用基于 HA 蛋白氨基酸模式的两个数学距离,将每种病毒与同一季节、亚型或谱系的相应 疫苗 目标毒株进行比较。我们计算了每个季节和亚型或谱系的平均距离,并将它们与已发表的临床 VE 估计值配对,在 13 个季节中产生了 26 个观察值。
主要结果
这两个数学距离均与历史 VE 呈负相关,表明流行病毒和 疫苗 毒株之间的较大差异与较低的 VE 相关。拓扑模型预测 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的 VE 分别为 40.3%、56.9% 和 47.1%,而几何模型预测相应的 VE 值为 34.0%、56.8% 和 51.1%。
解释与创新
使用数学人工智能方法,我们针对每个距离拟合了 VE 线性回归模型,并通过使用早期季节的数据预测后期季节来评估其预测性能,并以每个亚型或谱系的历史平均 VE 作为参考。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了一个预测框架,用于估计流感 疫苗 对特定亚型和谱系的有效性,这可以为 疫苗 毒株选择和公共卫生规划提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Mathematical AI Predicts 40% and 57% Influenza Vaccine Effectiveness Against H1N1 and H3N2, Respectively, for the 2026--2027 U.S. Season

Authors: Xiang Xiang Wang, Guo-Wei Wei, Wang, X. X.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Clinical influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) has often been low over the past two decades, highlighting the need for preseason VE forecasting. Timely forecasts could help health authorities plan prevention strategies and allocate healthcare resources. However, preseason forecasting of clinical influenza VE remains relatively uncommon. Methods: We obtained hemagglutinin (HA) sequences from GISAID for U.S. specimens collected in August--September before each influenza season from 2010--2011 through 2024--2025. After quality screening, we compared each virus with the corresponding vaccine target strain for the same season and subtype or lineage using two mathematical distances based on amino acid patterns in the HA protein. We calculated mean distances for each season and subtype or lineage and paired them with published clinical VE estimates, yielding 26 observations across 13 seasons. Using a mathematical AI approach, we fitted linear regression models of VE against each distance and evaluated their predictive performance by forecasting later seasons using data from earlier seasons, with the historical mean VE for each subtype or lineage as a reference. We then applied the same approach to 87 HA sequences collected in August--September 2026 to forecast VE for the 2026--2027 season. Results: We generated component-specific forecasts of influenza VE for the upcoming 2026--2027 U.S. Both mathematical distances were negatively associated with historical VE, indicating that greater differences between circulating viruses and vaccine strains were associated with lower VE. The topological model forecast VE of 40.3%, 56.9%, and 47.1% against A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B/Victoria, respectively, whereas the geometric model forecast corresponding VE values of 34.0%, 56.8%, and 51.1%.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Clinical influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) has often been low over the past two decades, highlighting the need for preseason VE forecasting.
Design and population
Timely forecasts could help health authorities plan prevention strategies and allocate healthcare resources.
Core methods
We obtained hemagglutinin (HA) sequences from GISAID for U.S. specimens collected in August--September before each influenza season from 2010--2011 through 2024--2025. After quality screening, we compared each virus with the corresponding vaccine target strain for the same season and subtype or lineage using two mathematical distances based on amino acid patterns in the HA protein. We calculated mean distances for each season and subtype or lineage and paired them with published clinical VE estimates, yielding 26 observations across 13 seasons.
Main results
Both mathematical distances were negatively associated with historical VE, indicating that greater differences between circulating viruses and vaccine strains were associated with lower VE. The topological model forecast VE of 40.3%, 56.9%, and 47.1% against A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B/Victoria, respectively, whereas the geometric model forecast corresponding VE values of 34.0%, 56.8%, and 51.1%.
Interpretation and novelty
Using a mathematical AI approach, we fitted linear regression models of VE against each distance and evaluated their predictive performance by forecasting later seasons using data from earlier seasons, with the historical mean VE for each subtype or lineage as a reference.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a predictive framework for estimating influenza vaccine effectiveness against specific subtypes and lineages, which could inform vaccine strain selection and public health planning.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

奥司他韦基有机盐和离子液体:理化性质、细胞毒性和神经氨酸酶抑制

Oseltamivir-Based Organic Salts and Ionic Liquids: Physicochemical Properties, Cytotoxicity, and Neuraminidase Inhibition
作者： Diogo A. Madeira, Vanessa Correia, Leandro J. dos Santos, Helena Rebelo‐de‐Andrade, Luís Cobra Branco, H. Rebelo-de-Andrade
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
神经氨酸酶 (NA) 抑制剂广泛用于流感 治疗,但耐药性的出现和药物配方的限制可能会影响其治疗效果。本研究调查了抗衡离子交换是否可以调节奥司他韦 (OS) 的理化性质、细胞毒性和 NA 抑制活性,同时保留其共价结构。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 NMR 和 ATR-FTIR 光谱合成和表征五种基于 OS 的离子制剂:[OS][C1SO3]、[OS][C3SO3]、[OS][C6SO3]、[OS][TolSO3] 和 [OS][CSA]。评估了制剂的水溶性、辛醇/水分配、对 MDCK-SIAT1 细胞的细胞毒性,以及对来自 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、其 H275Y 奥司他韦抗性变体、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B 型流感病毒的 NA 的 NA 抑制活性。
核心方法
这些制剂通过核磁共振和ATR-FTIR光谱法进行了合成和表征,其单独产率范围为84到88%。
主要结果
光谱数据证实了所需 1:1 盐的形成,并表明 OS 乙酯在化学上保持完整。所有制剂均表现出低细胞毒性,CC50 值高于 1200 μM。所有 OS 离子制剂对所有四种流感病毒的 IC50 值均低于磷酸奥司他韦,改善范围约为 2.5 至 393 倍。[OS][TolSO3] 显示出最高的效力,针对 H275Y 病毒 NA 的 IC50 降低了约 336 倍,针对乙型流感病毒 NA 的 IC50 降低了 393 倍。IC90 值显示出相同的总体效力排名,尽管 50% 抑制时的改善并不总是按比例维持在 90% 抑制时。
解释与创新
总体而言,抗衡离子的选择极大地影响了 OS 的物理化学和生物学特征。[OS][TolSO3] 提供最高的 NA 抑制效力,而 [OS][CSA] 表现出最平衡的整体特征,支持反离子工程作为优化基于奥司他韦的离子制剂的潜在方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,抗衡离子工程可能是一种有前景的策略,可增强基于奥司他韦的离子制剂对 季节性流感 病毒 治疗 的治疗潜力,特别是在解决耐药性和提高药物溶解度和活性方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外评估,不包括体内或临床数据。证据是中等的,因为结果基于实验室实验,没有考虑现实世界的变异性或长期影响。

Oseltamivir-Based Organic Salts and Ionic Liquids: Physicochemical Properties, Cytotoxicity, and Neuraminidase Inhibition

Authors: Diogo A. Madeira, Vanessa Correia, Leandro J. dos Santos, Helena Rebelo‐de‐Andrade, Luís Cobra Branco, H. Rebelo-de-Andrade
Original abstract (English)
Background: Neuraminidase (NA) inhibitors are widely used for the treatment of influenza, but the emergence of resistance and limitations in drug formulation can compromise their therapeutic performance. This study investigated whether counterion exchange can modulate the physicochemical properties, cytotoxicity, and NA-inhibitory activity of oseltamivir (OS) while preserving its covalent structure. Methods: Five OS-based ionic formulations, [OS][C1SO3], [OS][C3SO3], [OS][C6SO3], [OS][TolSO3], and [OS][CSA], were synthesized and characterized by NMR and ATR-FTIR spectroscopy, with isolated yields ranging from 84 to 88%. Spectroscopic data confirmed the formation of the desired 1:1 salts and showed that the OS ethyl ester remained chemically intact. The formulations were evaluated for aqueous solubility, octanol/water partitioning, cytotoxicity toward MDCK-SIAT1 cells, and NA-inhibitory activity using a MUNANA-based assay against NAs from A(H1N1)pdm09, its H275Y oseltamivir-resistant variant, A(H3N2), and type B influenza viruses. Results: All formulations exhibited low cytotoxicity, with CC50 values above 1200 μM. All OS ionic formulations showed lower IC50 values than oseltamivir phosphate against all four influenza viruses, with improvements ranging from approximately 2.5- to 393-fold. [OS][TolSO3] showed the highest potency, reducing the IC50 by approximately 336-fold against H275Y virus NA and 393-fold against influenza B virus NA. IC90 values showed the same overall potency ranking, although improvements at 50% inhibition were not always proportionally maintained at 90% inhibition. Conclusions: Overall, counterion selection substantially influenced both the physicochemical and biological profiles of OS. [OS][TolSO3] provided the highest NA-inhibitory potency, whereas [OS][CSA] exhibited the most balanced overall profile, supporting counterion engineering as a potential approach to optimize oseltamivir-based ionic formulations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Neuraminidase (NA) inhibitors are widely used for the treatment of influenza, but the emergence of resistance and limitations in drug formulation can compromise their therapeutic performance. This study investigated whether counterion exchange can modulate the physicochemical properties, cytotoxicity, and NA-inhibitory activity of oseltamivir (OS) while preserving its covalent structure.
Design and population
The study involved the synthesis and characterization of five OS-based ionic formulations, [OS][C1SO3], [OS][C3SO3], [OS][C6SO3], [OS][TolSO3], and [OS][CSA], using NMR and ATR-FTIR spectroscopy. The formulations were evaluated for their aqueous solubility, octanol/water partitioning, cytotoxicity toward MDCK-SIAT1 cells, and NA-inhibitory activity against NAs from A(H1N1)pdm09, its H275Y oseltamivir-resistant variant, A(H3N2), and type B influenza viruses.
Core methods
The formulations were synthesized and characterized by NMR and ATR-FTIR spectroscopy, with isolated yields ranging from 84 to 88%.
Main results
Spectroscopic data confirmed the formation of the desired 1:1 salts and showed that the OS ethyl ester remained chemically intact. All formulations exhibited low cytotoxicity, with CC50 values above 1200 μM. All OS ionic formulations showed lower IC50 values than oseltamivir phosphate against all four influenza viruses, with improvements ranging from approximately 2.5- to 393-fold. [OS][TolSO3] showed the highest potency, reducing the IC50 by approximately 336-fold against H275Y virus NA and 393-fold against influenza B virus NA. IC90 values showed the same overall potency ranking, although improvements at 50% inhibition were not always proportionally maintained at 90% inhibition.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, counterion selection substantially influenced both the physicochemical and biological profiles of OS. [OS][TolSO3] provided the highest NA-inhibitory potency, whereas [OS][CSA] exhibited the most balanced overall profile, supporting counterion engineering as a potential approach to optimize oseltamivir-based ionic formulations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that counterion engineering could be a promising strategy to enhance the therapeutic potential of oseltamivir-based ionic formulations for the treatment of seasonal influenza viruses, particularly in addressing resistance and improving drug solubility and activity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro assessments and does not include in vivo or clinical data. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on laboratory experiments and do not account for real-world variability or long-term effects.

流感 疫苗 2026–2027:季节较早,无需等待

Flu vaccines 2026–2027: An early season leaves no time to wait
作者： Jacek Z. Kubiak
摘要中文翻译实验室将在未来几周内对其进行测试,各机构应立即公布这些结果。研究人员应该测试 2026-2027 疫苗 诱导的抗体是否能中和 G155E 病毒,并以预印本的形式分享结果... Volume x Issue x (2026) 1 doi: 10.36922/JCTR026420119 编辑流感 疫苗 2026-2027:早期季节没有时间等待 Jacek Z. Kubiak1,2* 1雷恩遗传与发育研究所,UMR 6290 CNRS/雷恩大学,医学院,雷恩,法国 2分子肿瘤学和创新疗法实验室,军事医学研究所国家研究所 (WIM-PIB),Szaserow 128,Warszawa,波兰 临床杂志和临床与转化研究杂志转化研究 *通讯作者:Jacek Z. Kubiak (jacek.kubiak@univ-rennes1.fr) 引文:Kubiak JZ Flu 疫苗 2026–2027:季节提前,没有时间等待。doi: 10.36922/JCTR026420119 收稿日期: 2026 年 10 月 14 日 在线发布: 2026 年 10 月 22 日 版权所有:© 2026 作者。这是一篇根据知识共享署名许可条款分发的开放获取文章,允许在任何媒体上分发和复制,前提是正确引用了原始作品。出版商注:AccScience Publishing 对于已出版地图和机构隶属关系中的管辖权主张保持中立。北半球流感季节提前几周开始。这场流行病最初是由携带 突变 的 H1N1 病毒引起的,这让病毒学家和流行病学家感到担忧。这款专为 2026/2027 赛季准备的 疫苗 的所有三个组件均经过重建。这两个事实都指向同一个建议:我们应该比平常更早地接种疫苗,而且注射不应该是一个简单的形式。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 G155E 突变 的 H1N1 病毒的早期出现问题,这引起了病毒学家和流行病学家的担忧。该研究旨在就较早的 疫苗接种 提供建议,因为流感季节异常提前开始,英国和美国的阳性率较高。
设计与对象
该研究涉及美国流感病例的 监测,数据来自疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC),测序数据来自 Jesse Bloom 实验室。研究设计包括对西雅图的 H1N1 样本以及澳大利亚和美国的血液样本进行分析。
核心方法
进行测序以鉴定 H1N1 中的 G155E 突变,并在来自澳大利亚和美国的 260 个血液样本中测试了抗体识别。该研究还讨论了 2026/2027 流感 疫苗 的组成。
主要结果
截至 2026 年 9 月 26 日的一周,H1N1 占美国亚型阳性病例的 96%,未检测到乙型流感。测序指向 H1N1 中的 G155E 突变,并且某些样本中的抗体对某些携带 G155E 的毒株的识别效果较差。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为引起关注和进一步 监测 的原因,并指出新的 H1N1 毒株可能无法很好地覆盖携带 G155E 的病毒。他们强调 疫苗接种 的重要性,即使 疫苗 与循环菌株不完全匹配。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告不要推迟 疫苗接种,特别是对于 65 岁及以上人群、孕妇和卫生工作者等高危人群。该研究强调了及时 疫苗接种 的必要性以及新 疫苗 工具的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认基于鸡蛋和细胞的 疫苗 制造的局限性,它可能落后于病毒的变化。由于季节的早期阶段以及需要对 疫苗 针对新菌株进行进一步测试,证据强度中等。

Flu vaccines 2026–2027: An early season leaves no time to wait

Authors: Jacek Z. Kubiak
Original abstract (English)
Laboratories will test it in the coming weeks, and agencies should publish those results promptly. Researchers should test whether antibodies induced by the 2026–2027 vaccines neutralize G155E viruses and share results as preprin... Volume x Issue x (2026) 1 doi: 10.36922/JCTR026420119 EDITORIAL Flu vaccines 2026–2027: An early season leaves no time to wait Jacek Z. Kubiak1,2* 1Institute of Genetics and Development of Rennes, UMR 6290 CNRS/University of Rennes, Faculty of Medicine, Rennes, France 2Laboratory of Molecular Oncology and Innovative Therapies, Military Institute of Medicine-National Research Institute (WIM-PIB), Szaserow 128, Warszawa, Poland Journal of Clinical and Journal of Clinical and Translational Research Translational Research *Corresponding author: Jacek Z. Kubiak (jacek.kubiak@univ-rennes1.fr) Citation: Kubiak JZ Flu vaccines 2026–2027: An early season leaves no time to wait. doi: 10.36922/JCTR026420119 Received: October 14, 2026 Published online: October 22, 2026 Copyright: © 2026 Author(s). This is an Open-Access article distributed under the terms of the Creative Commons Attribution License, permitting distribution, and reproduction in any medium, provided the original work is properly cited. Publisher’s Note: AccScience Publishing remains neutral with regard to jurisdictional claims in published maps and institutional affiliations. The Northern Hemisphere flu season is starting weeks ahead of schedule. The very beginning of the epidemic is caused by an H1N1 virus carrying a mutation that worries virologists and epidemiologists. This vaccine prepared for the 2026/2027 season has been rebuilt in all three of its components. Both facts point to the same advice: we should vaccinate earlier than usual, and the shot should not be a simple formality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the early emergence of an H1N1 virus with a G155E mutation, which worries virologists and epidemiologists. The research aims to advise on earlier vaccination due to the unusual early start of the influenza season, with higher positivity rates observed in the UK and US.
Design and population
The study involves surveillance of influenza cases in the US, with data from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) and sequencing data from Jesse Bloom’s laboratory. The study design includes the analysis of H1N1 samples from Seattle and blood samples from Australia and the US.
Core methods
Sequencing was performed to identify the G155E mutation in H1N1, and antibody recognition was tested in 260 blood samples from Australia and the US. The study also discusses the composition of the 2026/2027 influenza vaccine.
Main results
In the week ending September 26, 2026, H1N1 accounted for 96% of subtyped positive cases in the US, with no detection of influenza B. Sequencing points to the G155E mutation in H1N1, and antibodies in some samples recognized some G155E-bearing strains less well.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a reason for attention and further surveillance, noting that the new H1N1 strain may not cover G155E-bearing viruses well. They emphasize the importance of vaccination, even if the vaccine is not fully matched to the circulating strain.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against delaying vaccination, especially for high-risk groups such as those aged 65 and over, pregnant women, and health workers. The study highlights the need for timely vaccination and the importance of new vaccine tools.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges the limitations of egg- and cell-based vaccine manufacturing, which can lag behind changes in the virus. The evidence strength is moderate due to the early stage of the season and the need for further testing of the vaccine against the new strain.

流感季节:诊断、预防和医学预防 — UMA REVISÓ SISTEMÁTICA

INFLUENZA SAZONAL: DIAGNÓSTICO, VIGILÂNCIA E PREVENÇÃO NA MEDICINA — UMA REVISÃO SISTEMÁTICA
作者： Cleiton Mendes Honorato Sousa, Lucas Moura do Nascimento, Matheus Eduardo Xavier dos Santos, Andderson Nascimento de Castro, William Lopes Soares Costa, José Cairo Cardoso da Silva
摘要中文翻译简介: 流感是一种由呼吸道感染引起的每年循环的流感,可响应医院的紧急运输、系统和客户的要求。目标:作为 2021 年和 2026 年公共科学证据的科学诊断、流行病学警戒和流感区域预防。方法:修订 PRISMA 2020 推荐系统,以 PubMed/MEDLINE 为基础,结合 MeSH 和人类流感相关术语、诊断、诊断和警戒疫苗接种。包括 4.922 项注册登记、2021 年和 2026 年期间的 70 项公共研究。结果: 第五次研究中的孔洞研究:临床和诊断方面;流行病学、基因组和预测模型的警戒;有效和推荐疫苗;Novas vacinais 平台,com destaque para as vacinas de RNA mensageiro;疫苗接种、犹豫和优先事项。每年一次的永久性疫苗接种主要是预防性的,根据不同的情况采取不同的有效措施,以防止病毒循环和预防。COVID-19 的大流行改变了流感循环的动态,包括 B/Yamagata 地区的流行病。结论:流感的控制取决于实验室诊断的综合时机、警戒和持续的基因检测、每年的疫苗接种和疫苗接种的犹豫,特别是妊娠期、妊娠期和新生儿期的疫苗接种。免疫受损。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目标:作为 2021 年和 2026 年公共科学证据的科学诊断、流行病学警戒和流感区域预防。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
方法:修订 PRISMA 2020 推荐系统,以 PubMed/MEDLINE 为基础,结合 MeSH 和人类流感相关术语、诊断、诊断和警戒疫苗接种。
主要结果
所提供的证据并未明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
COVID-19 的大流行改变了流感循环的动态,包括 B/Yamagata 地区的流行病。
科研与公卫意义
结论:流感的控制取决于实验室诊断的综合时机、警戒和持续的基因检测、每年的疫苗接种和疫苗接种的犹豫,特别是妊娠期、妊娠期和新生儿期的疫苗接种。免疫受损。
局限与证据强度
每年一次的永久性疫苗接种主要是预防性的,根据不同的情况采取不同的有效措施,以防止病毒循环和预防。

INFLUENZA SAZONAL: DIAGNÓSTICO, VIGILÂNCIA E PREVENÇÃO NA MEDICINA — UMA REVISÃO SISTEMÁTICA

Authors: Cleiton Mendes Honorato Sousa, Lucas Moura do Nascimento, Matheus Eduardo Xavier dos Santos, Andderson Nascimento de Castro, William Lopes Soares Costa, José Cairo Cardoso da Silva
Original abstract (English)
Introdução: A influenza sazonal é uma infecção respiratória aguda de circulação anual, responsável por elevada carga de hospitalizações, óbitos e custos aos sistemas de saúde. Objetivo: Sintetizar as evidências científicas publicadas entre 2021 e 2026 sobre diagnóstico, vigilância epidemiológica e prevenção da influenza sazonal. Metodologia: Revisão sistemática conduzida conforme as recomendações PRISMA 2020, com busca na base PubMed/MEDLINE por meio de estratégia booleana combinando descritores MeSH e termos livres relacionados à influenza humana, sazonalidade, diagnóstico, vigilância e vacinação. Dos 4.922 registros identificados, 70 estudos publicados entre 2021 e 2026 foram incluídos. Resultados: Os estudos foram agrupados em cinco eixos: aspectos clínicos e diagnósticos; vigilância epidemiológica, genômica e modelagem preditiva; efetividade e recomendações vacinais; novas plataformas vacinais, com destaque para as vacinas de RNA mensageiro; e cobertura vacinal, hesitação e grupos prioritários. A vacinação anual permanece como principal estratégia preventiva, embora sua efetividade varie conforme a temporada, o subtipo viral circulante e a faixa etária. A pandemia de COVID-19 alterou a dinâmica de circulação da influenza, incluindo o provável desaparecimento da linhagem B/Yamagata. Conclusão: O controle da influenza sazonal depende da integração entre diagnóstico laboratorial oportuno, vigilância sentinela e genômica contínua, vacinação anual com ampla cobertura e enfrentamento da hesitação vacinal, especialmente em gestantes, idosos, crianças e imunocomprometidos.

影响医护人员对流感 疫苗 接受度的因素

Factors Influencing Influenza Vaccine Acceptance Among Healthcare Workers
作者： Bekir Aktura, Esmehan Ayşit, Pınar Kıran
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在基于5C模型调查参与者决定接受或拒绝季节性流感 疫苗的心理过程。背景强调,流感是一种以急性呼吸道感染为特征的病毒性疾病。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究针对 181 名在初级保健机构积极工作且完整填写问卷的医护人员进行。参与者的平均年龄为 38.1 ± 7.2 岁,其中 75.1% 为女性,17.1% 为医生或专科医生。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集数据,评估与流感 疫苗接种 相关的社会人口特征、知识、态度和行为,以及七点李克特 5C 量表。在单变量分析中显示显着关联的变量随后被输入到多变量二元逻辑回归模型中。使用 Windows 社会科学统计包(版本 27.0)进行统计分析。
主要结果
总体而言,33.1% 的医护人员至少接种过一次 疫苗 流感疫苗,而在 2025-2026 季节期间只有 16.6% 的人接种过疫苗。至少接受过一次流感 疫苗 的参与者的集体责任感得分较高 (p = 0.045)。在当前季节接种疫苗的人具有较低的约束分数 (p = 0.006) 和较高的计算分数 (p = 0.030)。不同职业群体在自信、自满、约束和集体责任方面存在显着差异(分别为 p = 0.002、p < 0.001、p = 0.002 和 p = 0.022)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,无论是历史上还是 2025-2026 年季节,医护人员对流感 疫苗接种 的吸收率都很低。他们指出,推荐流感 疫苗接种 与 疫苗 接受程度独立相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以提高医护人员对流感 疫苗 的接受度,特别是通过解决集体责任和约束等心理因素。他们还强调了了解 疫苗 吸收的职业差异的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,不允许进行因果推断。证据是温和的,因为它基于自我报告的数据和一项调查,存在与响应率和参与者回忆相关的潜在偏差。

Factors Influencing Influenza Vaccine Acceptance Among Healthcare Workers

Authors: Bekir Aktura, Esmehan Ayşit, Pınar Kıran
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction This study aimed to assess attitudes and vaccination behaviors among primary healthcare workers toward seasonal influenza vaccination and to examine the psychological antecedents underlying their vaccination decisions using the Psychological Antecedents of Vaccination (5C) framework. Materials and Methods This cross-sectional study included 181 healthcare workers employed at primary healthcare institutions affiliated with a provincial health directorate. Results: The participants had a mean age of 38.1 ± 7.2 years, and 75.1% were women. Conclusion Influenza vaccination uptake among primary healthcare workers was low, both historically and during the 2025-2026 season. These findings support the need for multicomponent interventions that address psychological, behavioral, and practical barriers to vaccination while promoting collective responsibility among healthcare workers. A systematic review and meta-analysis estimated the pooled influenza vaccine uptake among nurses at 44%[15], whereas coverage among healthcare personnel in the United States reached 76.3% in the 2024-2025 season[16]. Interventions designed to improve vaccine uptake have produced heterogeneous results, and their effectiveness appears to depend strongly on the behavior-change methods employed[20, 21]. Data were collected using a structured questionnaire assessing sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors related to influenza vaccination, together with the seven-point Likert-type 5C scale. Participants who reported having received an influenza vaccine at least once in previous seasons were compared with those who had never been vaccinated. Participants were also compared according to their vaccination status during the 2025-2026 season. Variables showin...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the psychological processes underlying participants’ decisions to accept or reject the seasonal influenza vaccine based on the 5C model. The background highlights that influenza is a viral disease characterized by acute respiratory tract infection.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 181 healthcare workers actively working in primary care settings who fully completed the questionnaire. The participants had a mean age of 38.1 ± 7.2 years, with 75.1% being women and 17.1% being physicians or specialist physicians.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire assessing sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors related to influenza vaccination, together with the seven-point Likert-type 5C scale. Variables showing significant associations in univariable analyses were subsequently entered into a multivariable binary logistic regression model. Statistical analysis was performed using the Statistical Package for the Social Sciences for Windows (version 27.0).
Main results
Overall, 33.1% of the healthcare workers had received an influenza vaccine at least once, whereas only 16.6% had been vaccinated during the 2025-2026 season. Participants who had received an influenza vaccine at least once had higher collective responsibility scores (p = 0.045). Those vaccinated during the current season had lower constraints scores (p = 0.006) and higher calculation scores (p = 0.030). Significant differences across occupational groups were observed in confidence, complacency, constraints, and collective responsibility (p = 0.002, p < 0.001, p = 0.002, and p = 0.022, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the uptake of influenza vaccination among healthcare workers was low, both historically and during the 2025-2026 season. They noted that recommending influenza vaccination was independently associated with vaccine acceptance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions to improve influenza vaccine acceptance among healthcare workers, particularly by addressing psychological factors such as collective responsibility and constraints. They also highlight the importance of understanding occupational differences in vaccine uptake.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not allow for causal inferences. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on self-reported data and a single survey, with potential biases related to response rates and participant recall.

遗传血统塑造了南美洲原住民群体的环境依赖性免疫调节

Genetic ancestry shapes context-dependent immune regulation across Indigenous South American populations
作者： Choy, C., Vicuna, L., Patino, M., Araujo, D. S., Obispo, D., Guevara-Fujita, M. L., Fonseca, S., Scariot, D., Araujo, C., Guerreiro, J. R.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类 免疫应答 在南美洲原住民 (ISA) 人群中的特征仍然很差,限制了我们对人类免疫变异的理解。
设计与对象
Here, we generated a single-cell transcriptional atlas of 1.4 million peripheral blood mononuclear cells from 345 individuals representing 13 South American populations, profiled at baseline and following stimulation with lipopolysaccharide, influenza A virus, or Trypanosoma cruzi.
核心方法
遗传血统与大部分转录组的表达相关,通常仅在刺激后,并且解释了比年龄或性别更多的变异。
主要结果
平均而言,顺式 eQTL 占这些与祖先相关的差异的四分之一。
解释与创新
数千种监管变异在 ISA 人群中很常见,但在欧洲参考数据集中很少或不存在。
科研与公卫意义
将 eQTL 与 GWAS 整合揭示了刺激特异性疾病相关的调控变异,以及具有共定位信号的基因之间与祖先相关的表达差异的丰富。
局限与证据强度
我们的研究结果强调了研究跨免疫背景的不同人群以了解人类 免疫应答 的遗传结构的重要性。

Genetic ancestry shapes context-dependent immune regulation across Indigenous South American populations

Authors: Choy, C., Vicuna, L., Patino, M., Araujo, D. S., Obispo, D., Guevara-Fujita, M. L., Fonseca, S., Scariot, D., Araujo, C., Guerreiro, J. R.
Original abstract (English)
Human immune responses remain poorly characterized across Indigenous South American (ISA) populations, limiting our understanding of human immune variation. Here, we generated a single-cell transcriptional atlas of 1.4 million peripheral blood mononuclear cells from 345 individuals representing 13 South American populations, profiled at baseline and following stimulation with lipopolysaccharide, influenza A virus, or Trypanosoma cruzi. Genetic ancestry was associated with the expression of a large fraction of the transcriptome, often only after stimulation, and explained more variation than age or sex. On average, cis-eQTLs accounted for one-quarter of these ancestry-associated differences. Thousands of regulatory variants were common in ISA populations but rare or absent in European reference datasets. Integrating eQTLs with GWAS revealed stimulation-specific disease-associated regulatory variants and an enrichment of ancestry-associated expression differences among genes with colocalizing signals. Our findings highlight the importance of studying diverse populations across immune contexts to understand the genetic architecture of human immune responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human immune responses remain poorly characterized across Indigenous South American (ISA) populations, limiting our understanding of human immune variation.
Design and population
Here, we generated a single-cell transcriptional atlas of 1.4 million peripheral blood mononuclear cells from 345 individuals representing 13 South American populations, profiled at baseline and following stimulation with lipopolysaccharide, influenza A virus, or Trypanosoma cruzi.
Core methods
Genetic ancestry was associated with the expression of a large fraction of the transcriptome, often only after stimulation, and explained more variation than age or sex.
Main results
On average, cis-eQTLs accounted for one-quarter of these ancestry-associated differences.
Interpretation and novelty
Thousands of regulatory variants were common in ISA populations but rare or absent in European reference datasets.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating eQTLs with GWAS revealed stimulation-specific disease-associated regulatory variants and an enrichment of ancestry-associated expression differences among genes with colocalizing signals.
Limitations and evidence strength
Our findings highlight the importance of studying diverse populations across immune contexts to understand the genetic architecture of human immune responses.

流行性感冒 A 流行性感冒 H3N2 ในโรงเรยนแหงหนงอําเภอไพรบงจงหวดศรสะเกษ日期 13 日 - 3 日 2568

การสอบสวนการระบาดโรคไขหวดใหญชนด A สายพนธ H3N2 ในโรงเรยนแหงหนง อําเภอไพรบง จงหวดศรสะเกษ ระหวางวนท 13 กมภาพนธ - 3 มนาคม 2568
作者： Suwimon Seelueng, Priyada Klamngoen
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
通过审查病历并通过采访学生和学校工作人员积极发现病例,进行了描述性流行病学研究。
证据基础与方法
共确诊符合流感病例定义的病例43例,发病率为25.59%。
共识与结论
实施控制措施后,最后一例病例于 2025 年 2 月 25 日报告,在 监测 持续的 7 天内没有发现其他病例。
争议与缺口
A statistically significant protective factor was maintaining a distance of at least 1 meter between desks in classrooms (adj OR = 0.19, 95% CI: 0.06–0.58, p-value = 0.005).
科研与实践启示
目的是确认诊断和暴发的存在,描述问题的严重性并描述病例按人、时间和地点的分布,确定传播的感染源和模式,分析暴露个体的危险因素和保护因素,最终实施适当的预防和控制措施。

การสอบสวนการระบาดโรคไขหวดใหญชนด A สายพนธ H3N2 ในโรงเรยนแหงหนง อําเภอไพรบง จงหวดศรสะเกษ ระหวางวนท 13 กมภาพนธ - 3 มนาคม 2568

Authors: Suwimon Seelueng, Priyada Klamngoen
Original abstract (English)
An outbreak investigation of influenza was conducted in a school in Phrai Bueng District, Sisaket Province, during 13 February–3 March 2025. The objective were to confirm the diagnosis and the existence of the outbreak, characterize the magnitude of the problem and describe the distribution of cases by person, time, and place, identify the source of infection and modes of transmission, analyze risk factors among exposed individuals as well as protective factors and ultimately, to implement appropriate prevention and control measures. A descriptive epidemiological study was carried out by reviewing medical records and conducting active case finding through interviews with students and school staff. An analytical epidemiological study was performed to identify risk and protective factors among cases and controls. using a case–control study and Chi-square test, and Environmental assessment and laboratory investigations were also conducted. A total of 43 cases meeting the influenza case definition were identified, with an attack rate of 25.59%. The median age of cases was 11 years, and the male-to-female ratio was 1:1.38. Influenza A/B antigen rapid tests were positive for Influenza A in 3 cases, and real-time RT-PCR detected Influenza A/H3N2 viral RNA in 2 cases. A statistically significant protective factor was maintaining a distance of at least 1 meter between desks in classrooms (adj OR = 0.19, 95% CI: 0.06–0.58, p-value = 0.005). This study demonstrates that physical distancing, particularly in classroom settings, is an effective measure for reducing influenza transmission in schools. Following the implementation of control measures, the last case was reported on 25 February 2025, and no additional cases were identified during 7 days of continued surveillance.

迈向流感 疫苗 的普遍性:智能 疫苗 方案和佐剂驱动的生发中心反应。

Towards influenza vaccine universality: Smart vaccine regimens and adjuvant-driven germinal center responses.
作者： Arini Arsana, Wandi Zhu, Sang‐Moo Kang, Bao‐Zhong Wang
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 疫苗 每年都会重新配制,以匹配甲型/H1N1、甲型/H3N2 和乙型流感的流行毒株,但其功效仍然基本上仅限于中和针对毒株特异性但高度可变的血凝素 (HA) 头域的抗体反应。这种限制因预先存在的免疫而变得更加复杂,它优先回忆起针对主要免疫决定簇(主要是 HA 头域)的记忆 B 细胞,而不是相对保守的成分。相比之下,保守的病毒区域,包括 HA 茎、神经氨酸酶 (NA)、M2 胞外域 (M2e) 以及核蛋白 (NP) 和基质蛋白 1 (M1) 等内部蛋白,变异性要小得多,是广泛保护性免疫的有吸引力的靶点。开发有效且负担得起的通用流感 疫苗 取决于解决两个相互关联的问题:将生发中心 (GC) 选择重定向到保守表位,以及对 免疫应答 进行编程以实现持久的全身和粘膜保护。这篇简短的综述重点介绍了抗原设计、疫苗接种 策略和佐剂使用方面的最新进展。这些进展共同构成了开发通用流感 疫苗 的协调框架。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇简短的综述重点介绍了抗原设计、疫苗接种 策略和佐剂使用方面的最新进展。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。
共识与结论
开发有效且负担得起的通用流感 疫苗 取决于解决两个相互关联的问题:将生发中心 (GC) 选择重定向到保守表位,以及对 免疫应答 进行编程以实现持久的全身和粘膜保护。
争议与缺口
季节性流感 疫苗 每年都会重新配制,以匹配甲型/H1N1、甲型/H3N2 和乙型流感的流行毒株,但其功效仍然基本上仅限于中和针对毒株特异性但高度可变的血凝素 (HA) 头域的抗体反应。
科研与实践启示
这些进展共同构成了开发通用流感 疫苗 的协调框架。

Towards influenza vaccine universality: Smart vaccine regimens and adjuvant-driven germinal center responses.

Authors: Arini Arsana, Wandi Zhu, Sang‐Moo Kang, Bao‐Zhong Wang
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza vaccines are reformulated annually to match circulating strains of influenza A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and B, yet their efficacy remains fundamentally limited to neutralizing antibody responses against the strain-specific but highly variable hemagglutinin (HA) head domain. This limitation is compounded by pre-existing immunity, which preferentially recalls memory B cells targeting the predominant immune determinants, mainly the HA head domain, rather than the relatively conserved components. By contrast, conserved viral regions, including the HA stalk, neuraminidase (NA), the M2 ectodomain (M2e), and internal proteins such as nucleoprotein (NP) and matrix protein 1 (M1), are far less variable and represent attractive targets for broadly protective immunity. Developing an effective and affordable universal influenza vaccine hinges on solving two interconnected problems: redirecting germinal center (GC) selection toward conserved epitopes and programming immune responses for durable systemic and mucosal protection. This brief review highlights recent advances in antigen design, vaccination strategies, and adjuvant use. Together, these advances form a coordinated framework for developing a universal influenza vaccine.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This brief review highlights recent advances in antigen design, vaccination strategies, and adjuvant use.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Developing an effective and affordable universal influenza vaccine hinges on solving two interconnected problems: redirecting germinal center (GC) selection toward conserved epitopes and programming immune responses for durable systemic and mucosal protection.
Controversies and gaps
Seasonal influenza vaccines are reformulated annually to match circulating strains of influenza A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and B, yet their efficacy remains fundamentally limited to neutralizing antibody responses against the strain-specific but highly variable hemagglutinin (HA) head domain.
Research and practice implications
Together, these advances form a coordinated framework for developing a universal influenza vaccine.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

国家流感和 COVID-19 监测 报告:2026 年 10 月 22 日(第 43 周) - GOV.UK

National flu and COVID-19 surveillance report: 22 October 2026 (week 43) - GOV.UK
新闻简报2026 年 10 月 22 日(第 43 周)的全国流感和 COVID-19 监测 报告概述了英格兰的流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 活动。流感活动保持稳定,因流感样疾病急诊就诊的人数略有增加。与前一周相比,流感确诊病例数量有所减少。2026-2027 年季节,老年人、临床风险群体和幼儿的 疫苗 摄入量较低,但孕妇的摄入量较高。RSV 活性呈现出混合趋势,且处于基线水平。COVID-19 活动也好坏参半,医院病例稳定,急诊室因 COVID-19 类疾病就诊的人数略有增加。报告指出,流感 监测 已从 MEM 转向 MSD,并且前几周的数据质量问题得到了解决。USD 数据集用于 SARS-CoV-2 阳性计算,尽管存在一些方法学挑战,但总体 疫苗接种 吸收估计被认为是准确的。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该报告涵盖截至2026年第42周(即2026年10月12日至18日)的数据,发布日期为2026年10月22日。
地点与对象
该报告重点关注英格兰,提供了不同年龄组和人群的流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 活动数据,包括 75 岁及以上老年人、免疫系统较弱的个人和学龄儿童。
事件
该报告概述了英格兰流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 活动的现状,指出流感活动稳定、RSV 混合趋势以及 COVID-19 的低水平循环。它还讨论了 疫苗 吸收数据和测试方法。
规模、影响与风险
流感活动保持稳定并处于基线水平。流感样疾病的急诊就​​诊人数略有增加。流感确诊病例数较前一周有所减少。与上一季节相比,某些年龄组和临床风险组的 疫苗 摄入量较低,但孕妇的摄入量较高。RSV 活性呈现出混合趋势,且处于基线水平。COVID-19 活动呈现出复杂的趋势,医院病例稳定,急诊室因 COVID-19 类疾病就诊的人数略有增加。
应对、状态与不确定性
报告指出,流感实验室监测和GP拭子检测阳性已从使用MEM转变为使用MSD。第 41 周和第 42 周的 疫苗接种 数据存在不完整的问题,但这些问题在第 43 周得到了纠正。所使用的方法是实验性的,可能会在未来进行修订。统一样本数据集 (USD) 用于计算 SARS-CoV-2 测试阳性率,并且该数据对于总体 疫苗接种 摄取估计而言被认为是准确的。
A high priorityPublic-health news

National flu and COVID-19 surveillance report: 22 October 2026 (week 43) - GOV.UK

News Brief
The National Flu and COVID-19 Surveillance Report for 22 October 2026 (week 43) provides an overview of influenza, RSV, and COVID-19 activity in England. Influenza activity remained stable, with ED attendances for influenza-like illness increasing slightly. The number of influenza-confirmed ARI incidents decreased compared to the previous week. Vaccine uptake for the 2026-2027 season was lower among older adults, clinical risk groups, and young children, but higher among pregnant women. RSV activity showed mixed trends and was at baseline levels. COVID-19 activity was also mixed, with stable hospital cases and a slight rise in ED attendances for COVID-19-like illness. The report notes that influenza surveillance has shifted from MEM to MSD, and data quality issues in earlier weeks were resolved. The USD dataset is used for SARS-CoV-2 positivity calculations, and overall vaccination uptake estimates are considered accurate despite some methodological challenges.
Original source text (English)
This publication is licensed under the terms of the Open Government Licence v3.0 except where otherwise stated. To view this licence, visit nationalarchives.gov.uk/doc/open-government-licence/version/3 or write to the Information Policy Team, The National Archives, Kew, London TW9 4DU, or email: psi@nationalarchives.gov.uk . Where we have identified any third party copyright information you will need to obtain permission from the copyright holders concerned. This publication is available at https://www.gov.uk/government/statistics/national-flu-and-covid-19-surveillance-reports-2026-to-2027-season/national-flu-and-covid-19-surveillance-report-22-october-2026-week-43 This report summarises the information from the surveillance systems which are used to monitor COVID-19 (caused by severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2)), influenza, respiratory syncytial virus ( RSV ) and diseases caused by other seasonal respiratory viruses in England. The report is based on data up to week 42 of 2026 between 12 October and 18 October 2026. The main messages of this report are: Annual reports for the 2025 to 2026 season: Influenza in the UK, annual epidemiological report: winter 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Epidemiology of COVID-19 in England (published 25 June 2026) Seasonal influenza vaccine uptake in all GP patients: annual report 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Seasonal influenza vaccine uptake in school-aged children: annual report 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Seasonal influenza vaccine uptake in frontline healthcare workers: annual report 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Influenza activity remained stable and is circulating at baseline levels. Emergency department ( ED ) attendances for influenza-like illness ( ILI ) increased slightly at seasonally expected levels. The number of influenza-confirmed acute respiratory infection ( ARI ) incidents in the community decreased compared with the previous week. Weekly influenza vaccine uptake for the 2026 to 2027 season is reported for the third time this season. Data is for week 42 (up to 18 October 2026). Following issues with unavailable or incomplete influenza vaccination data at the time of analysis in weeks 41 and 42, several cohorts were not published in week 42. This has been corrected for this week’s report (week 43), and all cohorts are included. This will be corrected retrospectively in the report for week 42. There remains an issue that affects the attribution of vaccinations by provider setting, but is not expected to affect overall vaccination uptake estimates, which are considered to remain an accurate reflection of the influenza vaccination programme. Compared with the equivalent week last season (week 42 data, 2025 to 2026), vaccine uptake is lower among patients aged 65 years and over (44.1% vs 49.3%), patients aged 6 months to under 65 years in clinical risk groups (19.9% vs 22.1%), 2-year-olds (26.0% vs 26.9%), and 3-year-olds (25.8% vs 26.8%). In contrast, vaccine uptake is higher among pregnant women (26.0% vs 25.1%). COVID-19 activity showed mixed trends and is circulating at low levels. ED attendances for COVID-19-like illness increased slightly. The number of COVID-19 confirmed acute respiratory infections ( ARI ) incidents increased compared with the previous week. By the end of week 42 2026 (week ending 18 October 2026) 33.9% of all people aged 75 years and over, and 14.8% of all people aged under 75 years with a weakened immune system, had been vaccinated with an autumn 2026 dose since 1 October 2026. RSV activity showed mixed trends and is circulating at baseline levels. ED attendances for acute bronchiolitis increased and were at seasonally expected levels. Note 1: these indicators use the moving epidemic method ( MEM ) and the mean standard deviation method ( MSD ) to define thresholds to determine their respective levels of activity. Further information on these methods can be found in Influenza surveillance in Europe: establishing epidemic thresholds by the Moving Epidemic Method and Setting thresholds to determine COVID-19 activity levels using the mean standard deviation ( MSD ) method, England, 2022 to 2024 . The MEM approach is well-established for some influenza surveillance indicators, however, for other indicators both the MEM and MSD are experimental and may be subject to future revision. Influenza laboratory surveillance and GP swabbing positivity have transitioned from using MEM to using MSD . These approaches will be considered alongside expert opinion and triangulation of other data sources. The Second Generation Surveillance System ( SGSS ) captures test result information for notifiable infectious diseases, including COVID-19 and influenza, from laboratories in England. The unified sample dataset ( USD ) stores all SARS-CoV-2 test results reported to SGSS , Respiratory DataMart and UKHSA laboratories, and is used to calculate the percentage of tests positive for SARS-CoV-2 among all SARS-CoV-2 tests conducted. As of 20 October 2026, there were a total of 1,915 COVID-19 cases identified in hospital settings in week 42, remaining stable compared with 2,007 cases in the previous week. SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19) PCR positivity in hospital settings remained stable in week 42, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 8.9% up to Sunday 18 October 2026 compared with 8.8% for the same period on the previous Sunday. Positivity rates were highest among those aged 85 years and over with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 13.4% up to Sunday 18 October 2026. This has remained stable compared with 13.7% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. Note 2: data from previous seasons is aligned by day. Note 3: testing policy and practice may change over time which can impact positivity rates, therefore comparisons over time should be interpreted with caution. Notable changes in testing policy occurred during 2022 to 2023, which are outlined in the data quality report . Note 4: the highlighted line corresponds to the age group in the subplot title, grey lines correspond to all other age groups. Influenza positivity remained stable in week 42, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 6.3% up to Sunday 18 October 2026. This is compared with 6.2% on the previous Sunday. By type, influenza A positivity remained stable in week 42, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 5.5% up to Sunday 18 October 2026. Influenza B positivity decreased in the same week, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 0.2% up to Sunday 18 October 2026. Influenza A positivity rates were highest among those aged between 18 and 24 years, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 12.0% up to Sunday 18 October 2026. This has decreased from 16.8% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. Influenza B positivity rates for the same period were highest among those aged between 5 and 11 years, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 2.4%. This has decreased from 3.0% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. RSV swab-positivity based PCR test results reported through SGSS have been included as a pilot indicator from week 49 2025 onwards. RSV positivity increased in week 42, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 1.1% up to Sunday 18 October 2026, compared with 0.7% up to the previous Sunday. RSV positivity rates were highest in those aged between 0 and 4 years with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 7.8% up to Sunday 18 October 2026 in week 42. This has increased from 4.5% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. Respiratory DataMart is a sentinel laboratory-based surveillance system where participating laboratories report positive and negative test results for a number of respiratory viruses from samples primarily taken in hospital. A small proportion of primary care samples are also included in this reporting. In week 42, data is based on reporting from 9 out of the 14 sentinel laboratories. In week 42, 3,856 respiratory specimens reported through the Respiratory DataMart System were tested for influenza. There were 231 positive samples for influenza: 143 influenza A (not subtyped), 13 influenza A (H3N2), 47 influenza A (H1N1)pdm09, and 30 influenza B. Overall, influenza positivity decreased slightly to 6.0% in week 42 compared with 6.3% in the previous week. In week 42, 4,586 respiratory specimens reported through the Respiratory DataMart System were tested for SARS-CoV-2. There were 331 positive samples for SARS-CoV-2. SARS-CoV-2 positivity decreased slightly to 7.2% compared with 8.1% in the previous week, with the highest positivity in those aged 80 years and over at 10.2%. RSV positivity remained stable at 0.9%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 5.7%. Adenovirus positivity decreased slightly to 1.1%, with the highest positivity in those aged between 5 and 14 years at 4.6%. Human metapneumovirus ( hMPV ) positivity increased to 0.5%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 1.8%. Parainfluenza positivity increased to 3.4%, with the highest positivity in those aged between 5 and 14 years at 9.4%. Rhinovirus positivity decreased slightly to 17.1%, with the highest positivity in those aged between 5 and 14 years at 36.0%. DataMart data is provisional and subject to retrospective updates. Note 5: shading represents 95% confidence intervals. UKHSA conducts genomic surveillance of SARS-CoV-2 lineages. This section provides an overview of circulating lineages in England, derived from data on sequenced PCR -positive SARS-CoV-2 samples in SGSS . The prevalence of UKHSA -designated lineages among sequenced cases is presented in Figure 13. To account for reporting delays, we report the proportion of lineages within COVID-19 cases that have had a sequenced positive sample between 14 September 2026 and 27 September 2026. Note that low sequencing numbers, especially within the latest reporting period, will impact the accuracy of the prevalence estimates. These most recent figures should therefore be interpreted with caution. Note that lineages will be grouped independently from their parent lineage once they reach sufficient prevalence, and may be re-grouped into their parent lineage if their prevalence subsequently falls. The data quality report contains more information on lineage groupings. Analysis of viruses from primary and secondary care shows that circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses show low genetic diversity and all belong to clade D.3.1.1. Observations published from the WHO influenza vaccine composition meeting (VCM) in September 2026 suggest low reactivity of some viruses within the D.3.1.1 subclade with post-infection ferret antisera raised against A/Missouri/11/2025 (H1N1)pdm09-like virus – the A(H1N1)pdm09 component in 2026 Southern and 2026/2027 Northern Hemisphere cell- and egg-based influenza vaccines - and human sera post-vaccination with the Southern Hemisphere vaccine 2026. It is not yet clear how vaccine effectiveness against clinical disease may be affected by these observations. Vaccines typically still provide protection against severe clinical disease when drifted strains are seen with only a modest reduction in effectiveness. The characterisation of circulating A(H3N2) viruses shows that A(H3N2) viruses are currently showing low genetic diversity. All sequenced viruses to date belong to genetic subclade K. To date, only a small number of influenza B viruses have been detected, which show some genetic diversity. Between week 35 2026 (week ending 30 August 2026) and week 42 2026 (week ending 18 October 2026), the UKHSA respiratory virus unit ( RVU ) has genetically characterised 242 seasonal influenza viruses, and identified 148 influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses, 72 influenza A(H3N2) viruses, and 22 influenza B viruses. Details of the characterised viruses by subtype are shown in Tables 1, 2 and 3. The RVU has confirmed by genome sequencing the detection of live attenuated influenza vaccine ( LAIV ) viruses in 7 influenza A/B positive samples, collected from children aged between 2 and 16 years. UKHSA RVU performs antigenic characterisation of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, influenza A(H3N2) and influenza B viruses using haemagglutination inhibition ( HI ) assays. Data from these assays are used to compare how similar the currently circulating influenza viruses are to the strains included in seasonal influenza vaccines, and to monitor for changes in circulating influenza viruses. Similarity of currently circulating influenza strains to vaccine strains is defined as having an antibody titre within 4-fold when compared with reference viruses representative of the vaccine strain. Antigenic characterisation of viruses in the current reporting period is underway. A(H1N1)pdm09: 5 A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses have been antigenically characterised and 3 were similar to reference viruses representative of the A/Missouri/11/2025 (H1N1)pdm09-like Northern Hemisphere 2026/27 (H1N1)pdm09 vaccine strains. Influenza positive samples are screened for mutations in the virus neuraminidase ( NA ) and the cap-dependent endonuclease of the polymerase acidic protein (PA) genes known to confer neuraminidase inhibitor (oseltamivir and zanamivir) or baloxavir resistance, respectively. Results from this surveillance are given in table 4. There have been less than 5 detections with mutations related to reduced susceptibility/resistance to oseltamivir and/or reduced susceptibility to baloxavir. Data is presented on viral ARI incidents in different settings that are reported to UKHSA health protection teams ( HPTs ). Please note that reporting practices are known to vary between seasons and between regions. Any interpretation of temporal and regional trends should consider the likelihood of differences in reporting of ARI incidents over time and between regions. There were 135 new ARI incidents reported in week 42 in England. These included: 124 incidents from care homes, of which 43 were due to SARS-CoV-2, 11 were due to influenza A, 3 were due to influenza (no type information available), 3 were due to multiple pathogens, 1 was due to RSV and 1 was due to other pathogens. No pathogen was reported in 62 incidents 6 incidents from hospitals, of which 4 were due to SARS-CoV-2, 1 was due to influenza A and 1 was due to multiple pathogens 2 incidents from educational settings, of which 1 was due to RSV . No pathogen was reported in 1 incident 1 incident from prisons, of which 1 was due to SARS-CoV-2 2 incidents from other settings, of which 1 was due to SARS-CoV-2 and 1 was due to influenza (no type information available) Syndromic surveillance collects data from various healthcare sources where presentations are classified by patterns of symptoms compatible with specific infections. In some settings, the syndromic diagnosis can be supplemented by (rapid) testing. In this report, ED attendances are displayed. Further details and data from other syndromic surveillance systems can be found in the syndromic surveillance weekly summaries . During the week ending on 18 October 2026, ED attendances for acute respiratory infections increased and were similar to seasonally expected levels. ED attendances for COVID-19-like illness increased. ED attendance for influenza-like illness increased and was above seasonally expected levels. ED attendance for acute bronchiolitis (a syndrome related to RSV infection) increased and was below seasonally expected levels. Daily NHS 111 acute respiratory infection triaged calls showed small increases. GP in-hours COVID-19-like consultation rate continued to increase, mainly in those aged 65 years and over. Upper respiratory tract infections remained stable overall however there was a further small increase in influenza-like illness ( ILI ), which remained above seasonal expectations. ILI rates increased in adult age groups over 45 years. GP out-of-hours daily contacts for acute respiratory infections stabilised and remained in line with seasonally expected levels. Contacts for ‘difficulty breathing, wheeze, or asthma’ decreased but remained above seasonally expected levels. Note 6: 7-day moving average is adjusted for bank holidays. Grey columns show weekends and bank holidays. Note 7: the coloured line is the 7-day moving average by age group. Grey lines show the other age groups. The y-axis shows daily attendances. It is adjusted for bank holidays. Primary care surveillance is undertaken in collaboration with the Royal College of General Practitioners ( RCGP ) Research and Surveillance Centre ( RSC ), using a national sentinel surveillance system of around 2,000 GP practices covering over 20 million registered patients of all ages across England. More information on the methodology can be found in the data quality report . The weekly ILI consultation rate through the RCGP surveillance remained stable at 6.5 per 100,000 registered population in participating GP practices in week 42 compared with 6.3 per 100,000 in the previous week. This rate is in the baseline activity level (Figure 20). By age group, the highest rates were seen in those aged between 15 and 44 years (7.6 per 100,000), and those aged between 45 and 64 years (7.0 per 100,000). The lower respiratory tract infections ( LRTI ) consultation rate remained stable at 81.9 per 100,000 in week 42. Further details are available in the weekly RSC communicable and respiratory disease report for England . MEM thresholds are based on data from the 2018 to 2019 season to the 2025 to 2026 season. Note the 2019 to 2020, 2020 to 2021 and 2021 to 2022 seasons have been removed. From week 40 2025, the RCGP sentinel swabbing scheme testing capability has been expanded to the UKHSA Bristol laboratory in addition to the UKHSA Colindale laboratory. Samples sent to Colindale are tested for influenza A and B, RSV A and B, SARS-CoV-2, hMPV , adenovirus, seasonal coronavirus and enterovirus/rhinovirus while samples sent to Bristol are tested for influenza A and B, RSV and SARS-CoV-2. 931 samples were taken in...
View five news elements
Time
The report covers data up to week 42 of 2026, between 12 October and 18 October 2026, with the publication date being 22 October 2026.
Location and population
The report is focused on England, with data on influenza, RSV, and COVID-19 activity across different age groups and populations, including those aged 75 years and over, individuals with weakened immune systems, and school-aged children.
Event
The report outlines the current status of influenza, RSV, and COVID-19 activity in England, noting stable influenza activity, mixed trends for RSV, and low-level circulation of COVID-19. It also discusses vaccine uptake data and testing methodologies.
Scale, impact and risk
Influenza activity remained stable and at baseline levels. ED attendances for influenza-like illness increased slightly. The number of influenza-confirmed ARI incidents decreased compared with the previous week. Vaccine uptake was lower among certain age groups and clinical risk groups compared to the previous season, but higher among pregnant women. RSV activity showed mixed trends and was at baseline levels. COVID-19 activity showed mixed trends, with stable hospital cases and a slight increase in ED attendances for COVID-19-like illness.
Response, status and uncertainty
The report indicates that influenza laboratory surveillance and GP swabbing positivity have transitioned from using MEM to MSD. There were issues with incomplete vaccination data in weeks 41 and 42, but these were corrected in week 43. The methods used are experimental and may be subject to future revision. The unified sample dataset (USD) is used to calculate SARS-CoV-2 test positivity rates, and the data is considered accurate for overall vaccination uptake estimates.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

FDA 批准 Moderna 的 mRNA 季节性流感 疫苗 - ABC News - 突发新闻、最新新闻和视频

FDA approves Moderna's mRNA seasonal flu vaccine - ABC News - Breaking News, Latest News and Videos
新闻简报美国食品和药物管理局 (FDA) 于 2026 年 8 月 6 日 (N4) 批准 Moderna 的 mRNA 季节性流感 疫苗、mFLUSIVA 用于 50 岁以上成人。该批准最初被推迟,但在 Moderna 承诺进行更多研究后重新考虑(N15)。疫苗 预计将在 2026-2027 年呼吸季节上市,目标人群为 50 岁及以上 (N5) 人群。在一项涉及 50 至 64 岁人群的临床试验中,与标准 疫苗 (N10) 相比,mFLUSIVA 将流感确诊病例减少了 27%。安全性与标准 疫苗 相似,但更多人报告有轻微副作用 (N11)。疫苗 旨在降低严重疾病的风险,包括住院和死亡 (N25)。Moderna 计划在今年冬天监测其表现,并向 FDA 提交数据,以确定其继续批准用于老年人 (N13)。此次批准是解决 季节性流感 问题的更广泛战略的一部分,CDC 建议 6 个月以上 (N23) 的所有人使用流感 季节性流感。
查看新闻五要素
时间
美国食品和药物管理局于 2026 年 8 月 6 日星期三(N4)批准了 Moderna 的 mRNA 季节性流感 疫苗。该批准适用于 2026-2027 年呼吸系统疾病季节,疫苗 预计适用于 50 岁及以上人群(N5)。
地点与对象
该批准与美国相关,特别针对 50 岁及以上 (N5) 的个人。疫苗 是更广泛的 季节性流感 预防策略的一部分,其中包括针对 6 个月以上所有人的建议 (N23)。
事件
FDA 批准 Moderna 的 mRNA 季节性流感 疫苗(mFLUSIVA)用于 50 岁以上成人(N2)。该批准最初被推迟,但在 Moderna 承诺进行更多研究 (N15) 后重新考虑。
规模、影响与风险
在一项针对 50 至 64 岁人群的临床试验中,mRNA 疫苗 使确诊的流感病例比当前标准 疫苗 (N10) 减少了 27%。安全性与标准 疫苗 相似,但更多人报告有轻微副作用 (N11)。疫苗 预计将降低严重疾病的风险,包括住院和死亡 (N25)。
应对、状态与不确定性
Moderna 将在今年冬天继续监测 疫苗 的表现,并向 FDA 提交数据,以评估其对老年人的有效性(N13)。该批准是一个正在发展的故事,预计会有更新(N26)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

FDA approves Moderna's mRNA seasonal flu vaccine - ABC News - Breaking News, Latest News and Videos

News Brief
The U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA) approved Moderna's mRNA seasonal flu vaccine, mFLUSIVA, for use in adults over 50 on August 6, 2026 (N4). This approval was initially delayed but was reconsidered after Moderna promised additional studies (N15). The vaccine is expected to be available for the 2026-2027 respiratory season, targeting individuals aged 50 and older (N5). In a clinical trial involving people aged 50 to 64, mFLUSIVA reduced confirmed flu cases by 27% compared to the standard vaccine (N10). The safety profile was similar to the standard vaccine, though more people reported mild side effects (N11). The vaccine is designed to reduce the risk of severe illness, including hospitalization and death (N25). Moderna plans to monitor its performance this winter and submit data to the FDA to determine its continued approval for older adults (N13). The approval is part of a broader strategy to address seasonal influenza, with the CDC recommending flu vaccines for everyone over 6 months (N23).

IJZCG:针对季节性流感的 疫苗接种 将于 10 月 26 日开始 - vijesti.me

IJZCG: Vaccination against seasonal flu begins on October 26th - vijesti.me
新闻简报黑山公共卫生研究所 (IJZCG) 宣布于 2026 年 10 月 26 日开始启动 2026/2027 年季节性流感 疫苗接种。疫苗接种 活动主要针对高危人群,包括 65 岁以上的个人、慢性病患者、孕妇、医护人员以及居住在集体住宿或为弱势群体提供护理的人员。IJZCG强调,疫苗接种是降低重症、住院和死亡风险的最有效措施。该机构还强调了针对流感和其他呼吸道感染的一般防护措施的重要性,例如定期洗手、室内通风、避免与感染者密切接触以及必要时在封闭空间内佩戴​​口罩。此外,IJZCG表示,他们定期监测流感传播情况,并与卫生机构合作,及时应对流行病学情况。鼓励公民咨询当地卫生中心,了解有关 疫苗接种 地点和方法的详细信息。

时间
疫苗接种 于 2026 年 10 月 26 日开始(N1、N3)。发布于 2026 年 10 月 22 日 (N1)。
地点与对象
黑山,重点关注卫生中心和公民(N1、N3、N18)。
事件
黑山公共卫生研究所 (IJZCG) 宣布,2026/2027 年季节性流感 疫苗接种 将于 2026 年 10 月 26 日开始(N1、N3)。
规模、影响与风险
流感可引起严重的健康并发症,尤其是慢性病患者、老年人和其他敏感人群(N6)。
应对、状态与不确定性

B routine priorityPublic-health news

IJZCG: Vaccination against seasonal flu begins on October 26th - vijesti.me

News Brief
The Institute of Public Health of Montenegro (IJZCG) announced the start of seasonal flu vaccination for the 2026/2027 season on October 26, 2026. The vaccination campaign is recommended primarily for high-risk groups, including individuals over 65 years of age, people with chronic diseases, pregnant women, healthcare workers, and those living in collective accommodations or providing care to vulnerable individuals. IJZCG emphasized that vaccination is the most effective measure to reduce the risk of severe illness, hospitalization, and death. The institution also highlighted the importance of general protective measures against influenza and other respiratory infections, such as regular handwashing, room ventilation, avoiding close contact with infected individuals, and wearing masks in enclosed spaces when necessary. Additionally, IJZCG stated that they regularly monitor the spread of influenza and collaborate with health institutions to respond to the epidemiological situation promptly. Citizens are encouraged to consult their local health centers for details on vaccination locations and methods.
Original source text (English)

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

2026-2027 年流感季节:家庭流感测试、疫苗 和须知 - 罗切斯特大学

Flu Season 2026–2027: At-Home Flu Tests, Vaccines, and What to Know - University of Rochester
新闻简报The University of Rochester Medicine has published information about the upcoming 2026–2027 flu season, emphasizing the importance of vaccination and testing. The flu vaccine for this season has been updated to better match the influenza viruses expected to circulate, including an updated H3N2 component. Vaccination is recommended for most individuals aged 6 months and older, with September and October identified as the optimal months for getting a flu shot. At-home flu tests are available but have an accuracy rate of 70 to 80 percent, and a negative result does not rule out the flu. The article also highlights the differences between influenza A and B, noting that A is more likely to cause severe outbreaks. While the severity of the flu season and dominant viruses are not yet known, the vaccine aims to reduce the risk of severe illness, hospitalization, and complications. The article advises seeking urgent care for severe symptoms and mentions that antiviral medications like Tamiflu are most effective when started within the first 1–2 days of symptoms. It also notes that flu can occur alongside other respiratory illnesses such as COVID-19 and RSV, making testing essential for accurate diagnosis.
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026-2027年流感季节即将来临,建议9月和10月(N2、N4、N47)使用疫苗接种。
地点与对象
该文章由罗切斯特大学医学院发表,重点关注美国人口(N2、N13)。
事件
2026-2027 年流感 疫苗 已更新,以更好地匹配本季度预计传播的流感病毒,包括更新的 H3N2 成分(N3、N13、N14、N43)。
规模、影响与风险
流感季节和主要病毒的严重程度尚不清楚,但 疫苗 包括更新的毒株以预防预期的流行病毒(N17、N15)。
应对、状态与不确定性
建议大多数 6 个月及以上的人使用 疫苗接种,家庭流感测试可以帮助指导后续步骤,尽管它们不是 100% 准确(N24、N31、N32、N39、N42)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Flu Season 2026–2027: At-Home Flu Tests, Vaccines, and What to Know - University of Rochester

News Brief
The University of Rochester Medicine has published information about the upcoming 2026–2027 flu season, emphasizing the importance of vaccination and testing. The flu vaccine for this season has been updated to better match the influenza viruses expected to circulate, including an updated H3N2 component. Vaccination is recommended for most individuals aged 6 months and older, with September and October identified as the optimal months for getting a flu shot. At-home flu tests are available but have an accuracy rate of 70 to 80 percent, and a negative result does not rule out the flu. The article also highlights the differences between influenza A and B, noting that A is more likely to cause severe outbreaks. While the severity of the flu season and dominant viruses are not yet known, the vaccine aims to reduce the risk of severe illness, hospitalization, and complications. The article advises seeking urgent care for severe symptoms and mentions that antiviral medications like Tamiflu are most effective when started within the first 1–2 days of symptoms. It also notes that flu can occur alongside other respiratory illnesses such as COVID-19 and RSV, making testing essential for accurate diagnosis.
Original source text (English)
Skip to newsroom menu Skip to main content Patient Care Education Research Community Skip to main content Newsroom Publications Dialogue NeURoscience Nursing Rochester Medicine Strong Kids Pathology and Laboratory Medicine View All Publications Media Inquiries Images and Video Additional Resources About Us Archives Explore URMC Healthy Living Flu Season 2026–2027: At-Home Flu Tests, Vaccines, and What to Know Oct. 23, 2026 Facebook Twitter LinkedIn Email Skip breadcrumb URochester Medicine Home Newsroom Publications Healthy Living Flu Season 2026–2027: At-Home Flu Tests, Vaccines, and What to Know Flu season 2026–2027 is approaching, and now is a good time to prepare. Key Takeaways 2026–2027 flu vaccines are updated to better match the influenza viruses expected to circulate this season, including an updated H3N2 component. September and October are generally good times to get a flu shot. Vaccination can still provide protection later in the flu season. At-home flu tests can help you decide what to do next, but a negative test does not rule out the flu. Flu antiviral medications work best when started within the first 1–2 days of symptoms. Flu season is almost upon us. You may be wondering when to get a flu vaccine, whether at-home flu tests can help, and what to know about the influenza viruses expected to circulate this season. What’s actually different this year, and what should you do if you or your family gets sick? University of Rochester Medicine Infectious Disease expert Angela Branche, MD, breaks down what has changed for the 2026-2027 flu season, options for testing and treatment, and why vaccination remains important. What’s Different About the 2026–2027 Flu Season? For the 2026–2027 season, all three components of the U.S. flu vaccine have been updated from the previous season. The vaccine includes updated strains designed to protect against influenza viruses that are expected to circulate this season. One of the changes addresses influenza A(H3N2) viruses, including the subclade K viruses that circulated widely last year. It is too early to know exactly how severe the 2026–2027 flu season will be or which viruses will dominate. Flu activity can vary from year to year and from one community to another. See a Doctor at Home University of Rochester Medicine Virtual Urgent Care clinicians can use home test results to help guide next steps, lowering the risk of further exposure that can come with in-person urgent care. Get Care Now What’s the Difference Between Flu A and Flu B? There are two main types of seasonal influenza viruses that infect people: influenza A and influenza B. Influenza A is the type most often responsible for large outbreaks and severe seasons. It mutates more frequently and includes subtypes like H3N2. Influenza B usually circulates later in the season and tends to cause milder illness overall, though it can still be serious. What Are the Symptoms of Flu? Symptoms can vary, but common flu symptoms include: Fever and chills Muscle and body aches Severe fatigue Nausea, vomiting, or diarrhea (especially in children) Cough or shortness of breath Flu symptoms can overlap with other respiratory illnesses, including COVID-19 and RSV. Taking a flu test or talking with a health care provider can help determine the next step. What to Do If You Think You Have the Flu If you start feeling sick, it's important to get tested early. Flu antiviral medications, like Tamiflu, work best when started within the first 1-2 days of developing symptoms. Are At-Home Flu Tests Accurate? “At-home flu tests are about 70 to 80 percent accurate,” says Dr. Branche. “But a negative test doesn’t rule out the flu.” If your test is negative but your symptoms are severe or worsening, it’s essential to contact your healthcare provider or visit an urgent care facility. University of Rochester Medicine Virtual Urgent Care clinicians can use home test results to help guide next steps, lowering the risk of further exposure that can come with in-person urgent care. Have a positive flu test? Start a video visit now When Should I Seek Care for the Flu? Antiviral medications like oseltamivir (Tamiflu) work best when started early. Within the first 48 hours of symptoms, antiviral treatment can shorten illness and reduce severity for most people. High-risk patients—including pregnant people, older adults, those with chronic heart or lung disease, or weakened immune systems—should seek care even if symptoms started more than two days ago. Seek urgent care right away if you have: Trouble breathing or chest pain Persistent high fever Severe dehydration from vomiting or diarrhea Confusion or sudden dizziness Can You Have the Flu and COVID-19 at the Same Time? Yes, but it isn't common. Because flu, COVID-19, and RSV can cause similar symptoms, testing can help determine what's causing your illness. Flu Vaccines and Protection Flu vaccination is recommended every year for most people 6 months and older. The 2026–2027 flu vaccine has been updated to better match influenza viruses expected to circulate this season. “The flu vaccine may not prevent you from getting infected this year,” Branche says, “but it can still reduce your risk of severe illness, hospitalization, and complications.” Vaccines help the immune system build memory, working alongside protection from past infections. Even partial immunity can make symptoms milder and recovery faster. When Should I Get a Flu Shot? For most people who need one dose of the flu vaccine, September and October are generally the best times to get vaccinated. Ideally, you should get your flu shot by the end of October, but vaccination can still provide protection later in the flu season. Flu vaccines are safe, widely available, and can still provide meaningful protection throughout the season. A nasal spray flu vaccine is available for some healthy people ages 2 through 49. Ask your health care provider whether it's appropriate for you. It takes about two weeks for your body to build its immune response after vaccination. Can the Flu Vaccine Give You the Flu? No. The flu vaccine cannot give you influenza. Some people have mild side effects after vaccination, such as soreness, fatigue, or a low-grade fever. Can You Get Flu, COVID-19, and RSV Vaccines at the Same Time? You may be able to get more than one vaccine at the same time. Talk with your doctor to determine which vaccines you need and when to get them. Protecting Babies from RSV Infants can be protected from severe RSV through either vaccination during pregnancy or an RSV antibody given to the baby. Talk with your pediatrician or prenatal care provider about which option is best for your baby. Why Does Vaccine Research Matter? Influenza viruses change constantly, which is why flu vaccines are updated every year. Vaccines use a piece of the virus to train the immune system. Unfortunately, that same part of the virus mutates easily. Researchers at University of Rochester Medicine and around the world are working to improve vaccine technology, including studies exploring broader and longer-lasting protection. “It’s a constant battle of humans against viruses, and sometimes we win, and sometimes we don’t,” explains Branche. “That’s why developing new vaccine technologies is so important. There’s always a way to do better.” Cold, Flu, and RSV Guide As we move into winter, illnesses like flu, RSV, COVID-19, and stomach bugs become more common. Stay updated on flu, RSV, and other seasonal illnesses: symptoms, prevention tips, and when to seek care. Get the latest info Connect with us! The 2026–2027 flu season is approaching, with vaccination recommended in September and October (N2, N4, N47).
Location and population
The article is published by the University of Rochester Medicine, focusing on the U.S. population (N2, N13).
Event
The 2026–2027 flu vaccine has been updated to better match the influenza viruses expected to circulate this season, including an updated H3N2 component (N3, N13, N14, N43).
Scale, impact and risk
The severity of the flu season and dominant viruses are not yet known, but the vaccine includes updated strains to protect against expected circulating viruses (N17, N15).
Response, status and uncertainty
Vaccination is recommended for most people 6 months and older, and at-home flu tests can help guide next steps, though they are not 100% accurate (N24, N31, N32, N39, N42).

医生警告季节性流感会造成严重并发症 - vietnamnews.vn

Doctors warn of serious complications from seasonal flu - vietnamnews.vn
新闻简报越南医生对季节性流感相关的严重并发症发出警告,尤其是儿童和高危人群。河内国家儿童医院报告甲型流感病例大幅增加,自 10 月初以来每周约有 70 名儿童入院,是前几周的三倍。在胡志明市,急性呼吸道感染也有所增加。大多数患者在一周内康复,但可能会出现肺炎、呼吸衰竭和儿童癫痫发作等并发症。医学专家强调及时治疗、在指导下使用抗病毒药物药物、避免不必要的抗生素使用的重要性。他们还建议将年度流感 疫苗接种 作为一项关键的预防措施。目前正在密切监测局势,并对弱势群体中疾病的潜在严重程度表示担忧。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 10 月 21 日(发布日期),流感病例自 8 月下旬开始上升,最近几周有所增加。
地点与对象
河内和胡志明市,尤其影响五岁以下儿童和慢性病患者。
事件
医生警告季节性流感会引发严重并发症,包括肺炎、呼吸衰竭和儿童癫痫发作,同时指出大多数患者会在一周内康复。国家儿童医院报告甲型流感病例大幅增加,自 10 月初以来每周约有 70 名儿童入院。
规模、影响与风险

应对、状态与不确定性
